8
Discussion

In this paper, we have studied a suite of inferential procedures for tensor data in the presence of heteroskedastic, subgaussian noise. Our main results depend only on the structural properties of the underlying tensor, and our confidence intervals and regions are shown to be optimal for independent homoskedastic Gaussian noise. We have also seen how our results can be used in three different concrete applications, resulting in several interesting insights for these problems.

In future work, it would be interesting to study other types of structures beyond the Tucker decomposition. For example, can similar distributional theory and inference be obtained for tensors with low tensor train rank
(
Zhou et al., 2022
;
Cai et al., 2022c
)
or low with additional sparsity structure
(
Zhang and Han, 2019
)
? In our results a leading-order term of the form
\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}
manifested; it would be of interest to see if similar leading-order terms arise in these other settings. In addition, throughout all of this paper, we have assumed knowledge of the underlying ranks
r_{k}
; however, in practice, this is typically not known
a priori
. Therefore, a practical and interesting theoretical problem is to develop inferential tools when the rank is either over or under-specified.

Throughout this work we have assumed that the noise is subgaussian, meaning that it exhibits certain tail behavior. In many settings, such as network data, the noise satisfies other distributional assumptions (e.g., Bernoulli noise), so it would be useful to establish the statistical theory for other noise settings. Moreover, the tools in this paper require that there are no outliers; it would be of interest to study statistical inference for models permitting outlier (e.g., heavy-tailed) noise.
In
Auddy and Yuan (2022)
, the authors showed that Tensor SVD is suboptimal when the noise has a finite
\alpha
-th moment for some
2<\alpha<4
. They propose an alternative procedure based on sample splitting to address this issue. Extending their analysis to provide valid inferential guarantees in this regime would be of interest.

In addition, our results in the main paper require the condition number
\kappa
to be bounded, though we permit
\kappa
to grow slowly in our general results stated in
Appendix

A
. Nonetheless, the recent work
Zhou and Chen (2023)
proposes an intriguing subspace estimation procedure
Deflated-HeteroPCA
that is shown to be optimal in both
\ell_{2}
and
\ell_{2,\infty}
norm for unbalanced matrices, and they propose applying their algorithm as an initialization procedure for
HOOI
, showing optimal
\ell_{2}
error rates that are condition number free. It would be interesting to combine their procedure with our statistical theory to obtain theoretical guarantees that are independent of the condition number.

Finally, our theory requires that
p_{k}\asymp p
for all
k
. The work
Luo et al. (2021)
establishes sharp perturbation bounds for tensors of varying order
p_{k}
’s, resulting in different phenomena for different regimes depending on the order of
p_{k}
and the signal strength. In many settings, one does not have
p_{k}\asymp p
, so it would be of theoretical and practical interest to develop statistical theory under varying signal strengths and orders of
p_{k}
.

Appendix A
Appendix Structure and More General Theorems

In this section, we describe the structure of the rest of the appendix and the analysis. We then state the more general results where we allow
\kappa,\mu_{0}
to grow with
p
. Our results in the main paper are readily seen to be implied by these more general results.

The rest of the appendix is structured as follows. First, in the subsequent subsections, we present generalizations of our distributional theory results, namely, Theorems
14
,
16
,
17
, and
Theorem

18
, which are generalizations of Theorems
1
,
2
,
6
, and
10
respectively. Next, we present generalizations of our confidence interval and region validity, namely,
Theorem

19
and
Theorem

20
, generalizations of
Theorem

3
and
Theorem

7
respectively.
Appendix

B
sets the stage for our analysis, including stating results and introducing notation from
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
. In
Appendix

C
we prove our main distributional theory results for the estimated singular vectors, and
Appendix

D
is concerned with proving the distributional theory for the entries. In
Appendix

E
we prove the validity of our confidence intervals and regions.
Appendix

F
contains the proofs from
Section

5
, as well as more general statements of Theorems
12
and
Theorem

13
. Finally,
Appendix

G
contains a self-contained proof of both
Theorem

4
and
Theorem

8
, as well as proofs of
Theorems

5
and
9
. Additional simulation results are presented in
Appendix

H
.

A.1
Distributional Theory and Entrywise Consistency Generalizations

The following result generalizes
Theorem

1
to the setting where
\kappa
and
\mu_{0}
are permitted to grow.

Theorem 14

(Generalization of
Theorem

1
)
.

Suppose that
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim p^{1/2}
, that
\kappa^{2}\lesssim p^{1/4}
, that
\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\kappa p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
,
and that
\lambda/\sigma\leq\exp(cp)
for some small constant
c
. Let
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}
denote the estimated singular vectors from the output of
HOOI
(
Algorithm

1
) with
t\asymp{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\log(\frac{\lambda/\sigma}{C\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}})
iterations, initialized via
Algorithm

2
. Suppose
\mathbf{T}_{k}=\mathcal{M}_{k}(\mathcal{T})
has rank
r_{k}
singular value decomposition
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}
. Denote
\mathbf{Z}_{k}=\mathcal{M}_{k}(\mathcal{Z})
.
Then there exists an event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Theorem}\ \ref{thm:eigenvectornormality}}
with
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Theorem}\ \ref{thm:eigenvectornormality}})\geq 1-O(p^{-9})
such that on this event it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}+\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)},

where

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

The following result is needed for our entrywise distributional theory results.

Theorem 15
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

14
. Under the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{thm:eigenvectornormality}}}
it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)},

where

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}\big\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

The following result generalizes
Theorem

2
.

Theorem 16

(Generalization of
Theorem

2
)
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

14
. Let
\Sigma_{k}^{(m)}
denote the diagonal matrix of dimension
p_{-k}\times p_{-k}
, where the diagonal entries consist of the variances of
\mathcal{Z}_{mbc}
if
k=1
,
\mathcal{Z}_{amc}
if
k=2
, and
\mathcal{Z}_{abm}
if
k=3
. Define

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\Sigma^{(m)}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}.

Let
\mathcal{A}
denote the collection of all convex sets in
\mathbb{R}^{r_{k}}
, and let
Z
be an
r_{k}
-dimensional Gaussian random variable with the identity covariance matrix. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}}|

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\bigg)_{m\cdot}\in A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\{Z\in A\}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{r^{2}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}.

Therefore, asymptotic normality holds as long as
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}=o\big(p^{1/4}/\sqrt{\log(p)}\big)
. Furthermore, when
\kappa
and
\mu_{0}
are bounded, a sufficient condition for asymptotic normality is that
r=o(p^{1/6}/\log(p))
.

Next, the following result generalizes
Theorem

6
.

Theorem 17

(Generalization of
Theorem

6
)
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

14
, and suppose further that

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}.

Let
\Sigma_{1}^{(m)}\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{2}p_{3}\times p_{2}p_{3}}
be the diagonal matrix whose
(a-1)p_{3}+b
’th entry is the variance of the random variable
\mathcal{Z}_{mab}
, and let
\Sigma_{2}^{(m)}
and
\Sigma_{3}^{(m)}
be defined similarly. Assume that

\displaystyle\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa^{4}\mu_{0}^{6}r^{4}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{8}\kappa^{6}r^{4}\log^{2}(p)}{\lambda^{2}p}\bigg\}.

Define

\displaystyle s^{2}_{ijk}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(i)}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(j)}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(k)}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}.

Let
Z
denote a standard Gaussian random variable and let
\Phi
denote its cumulative distribution function. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}\leq t\bigg\}-\Phi(t)\bigg|

\displaystyle=o(1).

Finally, the following result generalizes
Theorem

10
.

Theorem 18

(Generalization of
Theorem

10
)
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

14
, and suppose that

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}.

Then the following bound holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}-\mathcal{T}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sigma\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

Consequently, when the following condition holds:

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\mu_{0}^{3}\kappa^{3}r^{5/2}\sqrt{p\log(p)},

the bound above reduces to

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}-\mathcal{T}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}.

In particular, this bound holds if
\mu_{0}=O(1)
and
\kappa^{3}r^{5/2}=o(p^{1/4})
.

A.2
Confidence Interval Validity

The following results generalize
Theorem

3
and
Theorem

7
respectively.

Theorem 19

(Generalization of
Theorem

3
)
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

14
. Suppose also that

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}.

(4)

In addition, assume that
\mu_{0}\frac{r^{2}}{p}=o(1)
and that

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gg\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log^{2}(p)r^{2}\sqrt{p}.

Let
\mathrm{C.R.}^{\alpha}_{k,m}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k})
denote the output of
Algorithm

3
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)_{m\cdot}\in\mathrm{C.R.}^{\alpha}_{k,m}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k})\bigg\}=1-\alpha-o(1).

Theorem 20

(Generalization of
Theorem

7
)
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

17
. Suppose further that

\displaystyle\left\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\gg\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}.

Let
\mathrm{C.I.}^{\alpha}_{ijk}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk})
denote the output of
Algorithm

4
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg(\mathcal{T}_{ijk}\in\mathrm{C.I.}^{\alpha}_{ijk}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk})\bigg)=1-\alpha-o(1).

Appendix B
Analysis Preliminaries

In this section we introduce notation and present several previous results concerning the output of
HOOI
from
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
. We also describe the dependencies of all of our main results.

B.1
Initial Bounds and the Leave-One-Out Sequence

Our analysis is based on the theory developed in
Agterberg et al. (2022)
. First we state several results concerning the output of Tensor SVD. Throughout our proofs we assume that
t
is taken to be
t_{0}+1
, with
t_{0}
as in
Theorem

21
below.

Theorem 21

(Restatement of Theorem 2
Agterberg et al. (2022)
)
.

Suppose
\mathcal{T}
is a Tucker low-rank tensor with incoherence parameter
\mu_{0}
and condition number
\kappa
. Suppose that
\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\kappa p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
and that
r_{k}\asymp r
. Suppose further that
\kappa^{2}\lesssim p^{1/4}
and that
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim p^{1/2}
. Then for
t\asymp{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\log(\frac{\lambda/\sigma}{C\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}})
it holds
with probability at least
1-p^{-10}
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda/\sigma}.

We now recall the definition of the leave-one-out sequences defined in
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
. We define
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{S,j-m}
as follows. First, let
\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{k-m}
be the
k
’th matricization of
\mathcal{Z}
with its
m
’th row set to zero, and let
\mathcal{Z}^{k-m}
be the corresponding tensor. We then define
\mathcal{Z}_{k}^{j-m}
as the matrix
\mathcal{M}_{k}(\mathcal{Z}^{j-m})
, which is the
k
’th matricization of the tensor
\mathcal{Z}
with entries assocciated to the
m
’th row of
\mathbf{Z}_{j}
set to zero. We then define
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(S,j-m)}
as the leading
r_{k}
eigenvectors of the matrix

\displaystyle\Gamma\big(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{k}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m})^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m})^{\top}\big).

We then define
\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}
inductively as follows. For a given iteration
t
, we set

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}=\begin{cases}\mathrm{SVD}_{r_{1}}\big(\mathbf{T}_{1}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,j-m)}}\big)&k=1;\\
\mathrm{SVD}_{r_{2}}\big(\mathbf{T}_{2}+\mathbf{Z}_{2}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,j-m)}}\big)&k=2;\\
\mathrm{SVD}_{r_{3}}\big(\mathbf{T}_{3}+\mathbf{Z}_{3}^{j-m}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{1}^{(t,j-m)}\otimes\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t,j-m)}}\big)&k=3.\end{cases}

B.2
Additional Notation

Finally, we define the following additional notation defined in
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
. We set
\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{t}
via

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{t}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}&k=1;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}&k=2;\\
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t)}}&k=3.\end{cases}

We define
\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}
similarly. We also define the terms

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{t}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}\big)^{-2};

\displaystyle\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{t}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}\big)^{-2},

representing the
linear error
and
quadratic error
respectively. We also define

\displaystyle\tau_{k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\|\mathbf{U}_{1}\|=1,\mathrm{rank}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})\leq 2r_{k+1}\\
\|\mathbf{U}_{2}\|=1,\mathrm{rank}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})\leq 2r_{k+2}\end{subarray}}\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{U}_{1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}}\bigg)\|;

\displaystyle\xi_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\widetilde{\xi}_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{j-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t,j-m}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\eta_{k}^{(t,j-m)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)})\|&k=1\\
\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=2\\
\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+1}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k+2}^{(t,j-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=3\end{cases}

\displaystyle\eta_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{cases}\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t-1)})\|&k=1\\
\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=2\\
\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+1},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)})\|+\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{k+2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)})\|&k=3.\end{cases}

Denote
\delta_{L}^{(k)}\coloneqq C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)}
, where
C_{0}
is some appropriately large constant, and let
\delta_{L}=C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\max}\log(p)}
. We will use the following events from
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
(where
\sigma=1
without loss of generality):

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\max_{k}\tau_{k}\leq C\sqrt{pr}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\text{ for all $t\leq t_{\max}$ and $1\leq k\leq 3$ }\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|\leq C\left(\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(p)}\right)\bigg\};

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}\bigg\|\leq C\left(r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\right)\bigg\};

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\max_{k}\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|\leq C\sqrt{p_{k}}\bigg\}.

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{j-m}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\{\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{k}^{t,j-m},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)})\|\leq\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\bigg)\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigcap_{t=1}^{t_{0}-1}\Bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\mathcal{E}^{t,k}_{2,\infty}\cap\bigcap_{j=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{j}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t,j}\Bigg\};

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,1}\cap\bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{k}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t_{0},1}\bigg\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t_{0},1}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,3}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,2}\cap\bigg\{\bigcap_{k=1}^{3}\bigcap_{m=1}^{p_{k}}\mathcal{E}_{k-m}^{t_{0},2}\bigg\}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2,\infty}^{t_{0},2}.

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}_{j-m}^{t,k}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\Bigg\{\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\leq c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{-k}}}.\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\bigcap\Bigg\{\|\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{t_{0},j-m}\|_{2,\infty}\leq c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{p_{j}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+2}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+1)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{j}p_{k+1}}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k+2)}}{\lambda}+\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}-1}}\bigg)+c\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{\sqrt{r_{-k}}}{\sqrt{p_{-k}}}.\Bigg\},

where
c
is some deterministic constant. These events are analyzed explicitly in
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
.

B.3
Initial Lemmas

Without loss of generality, throughout this section we assume that
\sigma=1
.

First, we record the following lemma concerning the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
from
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
.

Lemma 1
.

Let
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
be defined as above. Under the conditions of
Theorem

21
, it holds that
\mathbb{P}\{\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\}\geq 1-O(p^{-30})
.

Proof.

See the proof of Lemma 19 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
.
∎

We note with the choice of
t_{0}=C\log(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}})
, we have that

\displaystyle\log(2^{t_{0}})

\displaystyle=\log\bigg(2^{C\log(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}})}\bigg)=C\log(2)\log(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}})\geq\log\bigg(\frac{\lambda}{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{\min}\log(p)}}\bigg),

provided the constant
C
is sufficiently large. Hence it holds that
\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}
. Therefore, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
for this choice of
t_{0}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|\lesssim\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}.

(5)

In addition, for this choice of
t_{0}
, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\mathrm{main}}}^{t_{0}-1,k}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{(k)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{k}}}.

(6)

In addition, the following result characterizes the properties of the leave-one-out sequences.

Lemma 2
.

In the setting of
Theorem

21
, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,k}
it holds that for each
1\leq j\leq 3
and
1\leq k\leq 3
that

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{(t,k-m)})\|\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}.

Proof.

The proof of Theorem 2 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
shows that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0}-1,k}
, for all
t\leq t_{\max}\asymp C\log\bigg(\frac{\lambda}{C\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}\bigg)
, one has the bound

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j}^{(t)},\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{j}^{(t,k-m)})\|\leq\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}+\frac{1}{2^{t}}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{p_{j}}}.

For the choice of
t=C\log\bigg(\frac{\lambda}{C\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}\bigg)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{t}}\leq\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p_{k}\log(p)}}{\lambda},

which completes the proof, with the implicit constant
2C_{0}
.
∎

Finally, we record the following result concerning the empirical singular values
\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}
.

Lemma 3
.

Let
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}
denote the diagonal matrix of leading
r_{k}
nonzero singular values of
\mathbf{T}_{k}
, and let
\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}
denote the leading
r
singular values of
(\mathbf{T}_{k}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}.
Under the conditions of
Theorem

21
, tor all
t\geq 2
, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{{\mathrm{Good}}}
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}\big)^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\lambda}.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we prove the result for
k=1
. First, observe that on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
, it holds that under the assumptions
\lambda\geq C_{0}\kappa p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
and
r\lesssim p^{1/2}
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\|\lesssim\sqrt{pr}\leq\lambda/8.

As a result, letting
\mathbf{\widetilde{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)}
denote the singular values of the matrix
\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}
, Weyl’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widetilde{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)}-\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)}\|\leq\frac{\lambda}{8}.

Furthermore, since by definition
\mathbf{T}_{1}=\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{1}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{r_{1}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{U}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{r_{1}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\bigg)\lambda_{\min}\bigg(\big({\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}\big)^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{r_{1}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}\bigg)\lambda_{\min}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t)}\big)\lambda_{\min}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t)}\big).

Next, by Lemma 1 of
Cai and Zhang (2018)
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t)})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=1-\lambda_{\min}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t)}\big)^{2}

which implies that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t)}\big)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{1-\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t)})\|^{2}}\geq\sqrt{1-\frac{15}{64}}\geq\frac{7}{8},

where we have used the fact that by (
5
), on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
one has

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta({\mathbf{U}}_{2},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t)})\|\lesssim\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}\leq\frac{3}{8}\leq\sqrt{\frac{15}{64}}

since
\lambda\gg\delta_{\mathrm{L}}=C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}
by assumption. By a similar argument, it holds that
\lambda_{\min}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t)}\big)\geq\frac{7}{8}
.
Therefore, this demonstrates that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{1}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg)\geq\lambda_{r_{1}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}\bigg)\frac{49}{64}\geq\lambda_{r_{1}}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}\bigg)\frac{3}{4}.

Consequently, combining these bounds, we see that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{1}}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)}\big)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{r_{1}}\big(\mathbf{T}_{1}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}\big)-\|\mathbf{\widetilde{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)}-\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)}\|\geq\frac{5}{8}\lambda.

As a result, one has that
\|(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)})^{-1}\|\leq\frac{2}{\lambda}
as required.
∎

Appendix C
Proof of Distributional Guarantees for the Loadings (Theorem
14
, Theorem
16
, and Theorem
15
)

This section contains the proof of
Theorem

14
,
Theorem

16
, and
Theorem

15
. The following subsection introduces the auxiliary lemmas needed for the proofs,
Section

C.2
contains the proof of
Theorem

14
,
Section

C.4
contains the proof of
Theorem

16
, and
Section

C.3
contains the proof of
Theorem

15
.
Throughout we assume that
t=t_{0}+1
, where
t_{0}
is such that
Theorem

21
holds. Throughout this section we assume without loss of generality that
\sigma=1
.

C.1
Preliminary Lemmas: First Order Approximations

In this section we present several lemmas that are useful for the proofs of the main results in this section. The proofs are deferred to
Section

C.5
. We assume throughout this section without loss of generality that
\sigma=1
.

The following result shows that the linear-term approximation is sufficiently strong.

Lemma 4

(Linear term approximation)
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

14
, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}-(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top})\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}^{(t)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}\bigg)\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}.

The next lemma shows that the contribution of the projection onto
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}
is sufficiently small.

Lemma 5

(Small Projection)
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

14
it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

The next lemma replaces the empirical linear term with the population linear term.

Lemma 6

(Replacing the empirical linear term with the population linear term)
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

14
, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg(\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}-\mathcal{P}_{k}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}.

Finally, the following result shows that
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}
and
(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}
approximately commute.

Lemma 7

(Approximate commutation of
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}
and
(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}
)
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

14
, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\sqrt{pr}.

C.2
Proof of
Theorem

14

With the lemmas from the previous section in place, we are now prepared to prove
Theorem

14
. Again without loss of generality we assume
\sigma=1
.

Proof of
Theorem

14
.

First, we note that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle e_{m}^{\top}

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{lem:linearapprox}}}}{=}e_{m}^{\top}(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top})\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)+\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{uperplemma}}}}{=}e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)+\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{lem:empiricallinearreplacement}}}}{=}e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{P}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)+\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\qquad+e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}\bigg)(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)+\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}+e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}\bigg)(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)+\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

Note that by Lemma 16 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
it holds that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
that

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim p\sqrt{\log(p)}\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}.

Furthermore, suppose
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}
has singular value decomposition
\mathbf{W}_{1}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{\top}
.
Then since
\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}=\mathrm{sgn}({\mathbf{U}}_{k},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)})
, we have
\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}=\mathbf{W}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{\top}
, and
\mathbf{\Sigma}
contains the diagonal entries equal to
\cos\theta_{i}
, with
\theta_{i}
the canonical angles between the subspace spanned by
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}
(see, e.g,
Kato (2013)
) and hence it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{W}_{1}\mathbf{\Sigma}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{\top}-\mathbf{W}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{\top}\|=\|\mathbf{\Sigma}-\mathbf{I}\|

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq i\leq r}1-\cos\theta_{i}\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq r}1-\cos^{2}\theta_{i}=\max_{1\leq i\leq r}\sin^{2}\theta_{i}=\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|^{2}.

(7)

Therefore, it holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
that

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{sinthetaegood}}}}{\lesssim}\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}p\sqrt{r}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{3}},

and that

\displaystyle\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}\bigg)(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}\|\|(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{lem:eigengaps}}}}{\lesssim}\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda^{3}}\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}\|

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{lem:approximatecommute}}}}{\lesssim}\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda^{3}}\lambda_{1}\sqrt{pr}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa r\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}.

Therefore, we have shown that there is an event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{VeryGood}}
with
\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{VeryGood}}\big)\geq 1-O(p^{-9})
such that on this event

\displaystyle e_{m}^{\top}

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa r\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}p\sqrt{r}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{3}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)+\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

where we have used the fact that
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{pr\log(p)}
, which holds under the conditions of
Theorem

14
since
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim\sqrt{p}
.
Therefore, by rotational invariance of vector norms, it holds that on this event

\displaystyle e_{m}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

This completes the proof.
∎

C.3
Proof of
Theorem

15

Proof of
Theorem

15
.

Again we assume that
\sigma=1
without loss of generality. We suppress the dependence of
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}
on
t
for convenience. Denoting
\mathbf{W}_{k}
as the orthogonal matrix in
Theorem

14
, by
Theorem

14
on
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Theorem}\ \ref{thm:eigenvectornormality}}
it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k})^{\top}+(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}){\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k})^{\top}+(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}){\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k})(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k})^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k})^{\top}+(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}){\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}+\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}+\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbf{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}+\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top}+(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}+\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}){\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-2}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top}+\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle\coloneqq{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}.

We now show the bound on
\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}
holds. We need to bound the terms

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-2}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}_{3}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}_{4}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}_{5}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}_{6}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}.

Note that by the proof of
Theorem

14
it holds on
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Theorem}\ \ref{thm:eigenvectornormality}}
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{{}^{-1}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda};

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{p},

where the second inequality holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
(which is included implicitly in the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Theorem}\ \ref{thm:eigenvectornormality}}
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{1}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|_{2,\infty}\frac{\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\frac{\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}.

The second term can be bounded directly by noting that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{2}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}\frac{\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\ll\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}.

Next,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\sqrt{p}\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sqrt{rp\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}},

since
\mu_{0}^{2}r\leq\sqrt{p}
and
\lambda\gtrsim\kappa p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
. Next,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\Phi}^{(k)}_{4}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}\sqrt{p}\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)\sqrt{p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}.

Next,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{5}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)})^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{p}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

The bound on
\|\mathbf{\Phi}_{6}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}
holds from the same bound as
\|\mathbf{\Psi}^{(k)}\|_{2,\infty}
, which completes the proof.
∎

C.4
Proof of
Theorem

16

Proof.

By
Theorem

14
, it holds that

\displaystyle e_{m}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa\sigma}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

It is straightforward to verify that the covariance of the vector
e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}
is given by

\displaystyle\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\Sigma^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}=\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}.

It is also straightforward to note that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\bigg(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\min_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{k}}:\|x\|=1}\|(\Sigma^{(m)}_{k})^{1/2}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}x\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\min}^{2}\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}x\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}.

Hence
\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}
is invertible, and its inverse has maximum eigenvalue at most
\lambda^{2}/\sigma_{\min}^{2}\lesssim\lambda^{2}\sigma^{2}
by Assumption
1
. Therefore, we have that

\displaystyle e_{m}^{\top}\bigg(

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)})-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\bigg)\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}\|(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\|(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

We will apply Corollary 2.2 of
Shao and Zhang (2022)
, with (in their notation)

\displaystyle\xi_{l}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{Z}_{k})_{ml}\bigg(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}\bigg)_{l\cdot};

\displaystyle\Delta^{(l)}=\Delta

\displaystyle=C\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

By Corollary 2.2 of
Shao and Zhang (2022)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}}|

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{e_{m}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\bigg)(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\in A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\{Z\in A\}|

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{1/2}\sum_{l=1}^{p_{-k}}\mathbb{E}\|(\mathbf{Z}_{k})_{ml}(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2})_{l\cdot}\|^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}\bigg)\mathbb{E}\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\|+O(p^{-9}),

where
\mathcal{A}
is the collection of convex sets in
\mathbb{R}^{r}
and
Z
is an
r
-dimensional Gaussian random vector with identity covariance. To bound the first term, observe that by subgaussianity (Proposition 2.5.2 of
Vershynin (2018)
),

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{p_{-k}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|(\mathbf{Z}_{k})_{ml}(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2})_{l\cdot}\|^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{3}\sum_{l=1}^{p_{-k}}\|(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2})_{l\cdot}\|^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{3}\max_{l}\|(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2})_{l\cdot})\|\sum_{l=1}^{p_{-k}}\|(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2})_{l\cdot}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{3}\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\frac{\|(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\|}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\lambda/\sigma_{\min}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\|_{F}^{2}\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}}r\frac{\lambda^{2}/\sigma_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}}{\sigma_{\min}^{3}}\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}}{p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{r^{3/2}}{p},

where we have used the fact that
\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\|^{2}_{F}=r
and that
\sigma/\sigma_{\min}=O(1)
. In addition, we note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r},

since the vector
e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}
is isotropic and subgaussian. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}}|

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{e_{m}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\bigg)(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\in A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\{Z\in A\}|

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{1/2}\sum_{l=1}^{p_{-k}}\mathbb{E}\|(\mathbf{Z}_{k})_{ml}(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2})_{l\cdot}\|^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}\bigg)\mathbb{E}\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\|+O(p^{-9})

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{r^{2}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}+O(p^{-9})

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{r^{2}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}.

This completes the proof.
∎

C.5
Proofs of Preliminary Lemmas from
Section

C.1

This section contains all of the proofs from
Section

C.1
.

C.5.1
Proof of
Lemma

4

Proof of
Lemma

4
.

First, the following expansion holds:

\displaystyle e_{m}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}-(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top})\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}^{(t)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}+e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}),

where
\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}
is the quadratic term defined in
Section

B.2

(see the proof of Theorem 2 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
for details on this expansion)
.
Without loss of generality consider the case
k=1
.
Recall we assume
t_{0}
is such that
Theorem

21
holds. For
t=t_{0}+1
, where
t_{0}
is such that
Theorem

21
holds, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t,1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
, the following bounds hold:

\displaystyle\tau_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{pr};

\displaystyle\eta_{1}^{(t_{0},1-m)}

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{sinthetaloo14}}}}{\lesssim}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda};

\displaystyle\eta_{1}^{(t_{0})}

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{sinthetaegood}}}}{\lesssim}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda};

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t_{0}-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(t_{0}-1)})\|

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{sinthetaegood}}}}{\lesssim}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda};

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim r+\sqrt{\log(p)}.

Next by Lemma 5 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(t)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg(\tau_{1}\eta_{1}^{(t)}+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}\big]\|\bigg)+\frac{16}{\lambda^{2}}\tau_{1}^{2}\eta_{k}^{(t,k-m)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4}{\lambda^{2}}\xi_{1}^{t,1-m}\bigg(\tau_{k}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|+\tau_{k}\eta_{k}^{(t-1)}+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big[\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}\big]\|\bigg),

where the notation is defined in
Section

B.2
, and the bound above holds whenever
\lambda/2\leq\lambda_{r_{1}}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}\big)
, which holds by
Lemma

3
on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
. Combining this bound with the bounds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t,1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
we obtain

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\sqrt{pr}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg)+\frac{pr}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}};

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\xi_{1}^{t,1-m}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\sqrt{pr}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg),

where we recall

\displaystyle\xi_{1}^{t,1-m}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{1-m}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg)\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t_{0},1-m}\bigg\|.

By Lemma 11 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)

and the fact that
\frac{1}{2^{t_{0}}}\leq\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}
, on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}
it holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
that

\displaystyle\xi_{1}^{t,1-m}

\displaystyle\lesssim r\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)},

where their proof of that lemma uses the fact that the events
\mathcal{\widetilde{E}}
defined in
Section

B.2
have empty intersection with the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}
.
Recalling
\delta_{\mathrm{L}}=C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}
and simplifying these bounds yields

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{t_{0}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}}{\lambda^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\bigg(\sqrt{pr}\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg)+\frac{pr}{\lambda^{2}}\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}};

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{r\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\sqrt{pr}\frac{C_{0}\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}+r+\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{3}}+\mu_{0}\frac{r^{3/2}+\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\sqrt{p}\lambda^{2}}+\frac{pr^{3/2}\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda^{3}};

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+r\mu_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\frac{r+\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)}{\lambda^{2}}.

Similarly, it holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
that

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}-\mathbf{W}_{1}^{(t)}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{sintheta14} and \eqref{sinthetaegood}}}{\lesssim}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}.

Combining these bounds and taking a union bound over all
p_{1}
rows yields that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
that

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)}-(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top})\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}^{(t)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-2}\bigg)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{(t)}\|+\|e_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}-\mathbf{W}_{k}^{(t)})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}p\log(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}\big(r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}+r\log(p)\big)}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{pr}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}

which completes the proof.
∎

C.5.2
Proof of
Lemma

5

Proof of
Lemma

5
.

Observe that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\|_{2\infty}\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\|\|(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{-2}\|\bigg(\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big(\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\big)\mathbf{V}_{k}\|+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big(\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\big)\mathbf{V}_{k}\|+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big(\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}}\big)\|+\sqrt{r}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg(\sqrt{rp}\frac{\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\sqrt{r}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa^{2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\mu_{0}r\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}},

where we have implicitly used the fact that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big(

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big(\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}\otimes\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(t)}_{k+2}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)}}\bigg)\|+\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(t)}_{k+1}}\bigg)\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(t)}_{k+2}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{k}\bigg(\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1},{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2},{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2})\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{pr}\frac{\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda},

where the final bound holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}

by (
5
)
.
∎

C.5.3
Proof of
Lemma

6

Proof of
Lemma

6
.

Without loss of generality, we consider
k=1
. On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t,1}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}-\mathcal{P}_{1}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{1}^{(t)})^{-2}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,1-m}-\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,1-m}-\mathcal{P}_{1}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\coloneqq(I)+(II).

For term
(I)
, we note that

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,1-m}-\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{1}^{(t)}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}}^{(t-1,1-m)}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}}^{(t-1,1-m)}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}}\bigg]\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\tau_{k}}{\lambda}\bigg(\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\scalebox{.6}{\text{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{sinthetaloo14}}}}{\lesssim}\frac{\kappa\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}r\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}},

where the penultimate bound holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}
.
Similarly, for term
(II)
, by Lemma 16 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
, it holds that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-30})
that

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,1-m}-\mathcal{P}_{1}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}p\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,1-m}-\mathcal{P}_{1}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}.

We now observe that

\displaystyle\|\bigg(\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,1-m}-\mathcal{P}_{1}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\otimes\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg\|\bigg(\bigg[\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\bigg]\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\bigg]\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\|_{2,\infty}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\|_{2,\infty}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\|_{2,\infty}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}.

It is straightforward to check that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\|_{2,\infty}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{2})\|+\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\mathbf{W}_{2}^{(t-1)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda},

where the final bound holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{main}}^{t_{0},1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
by
Lemmas

2
and
5
.
The same bounds holds with
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}
in place of
\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}
. Plugging this bound in yields that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-11})
,

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}p\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg\|\bigg(\mathcal{\widetilde{P}}_{1}^{t,1-m}-\mathcal{P}_{1}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}p\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg(\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{3}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\|_{2,\infty}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{\widetilde{U}}_{2}^{(t-1,1-m)}}-\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\|_{2,\infty}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa}{\lambda}p\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg(\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}.

Combining our bounds for both
(I)
and
(II)
, we obtain that

\displaystyle(I)+(II)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\kappa^{2}r\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}.

These bounds hold cumulatively with probability at most
1-O(p^{-10})
, and the proof is completed by taking a union bound over all
p_{1}
rows.
∎

C.5.4
Proof of
Lemma

7

Proof of
Lemma

7
.

First, by the eigenvector-eigenvalue equation, it holds that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k};

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)})^{2}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{k}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}-\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg)\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{T}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg\|+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg\|+\bigg\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}^{2}\frac{\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\lambda_{1}\tau_{k}+\tau_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}+\lambda_{1}\sqrt{pr}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\sqrt{pr},

where the final inequality holds when
\lambda_{1}\gtrsim\sqrt{p\log(p)}
together with the assumption that
\kappa\leq p^{1/4}
.
In addition, we have implicitly used the bound

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2}}}-\mathcal{\widehat{P}}_{k}^{(t)}\|\leq\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1})\|+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{k+2})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\delta_{\mathrm{L}}}{\lambda},

which holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
by (
5
).

∎

Appendix D
Proof of Entrywise Distributional Theory (Theorem
17
) and
\|\cdot\|_{\max}
Convergence (Corollary
18
)

This section contains the proofs of the entrywise distributional theory and entrywise
\|\cdot\|_{\max}
convergence. Throughout this section we suppress the dependence of
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{(t)}
, and
\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{(t)}
on
t
.
Section

D.1
gives preliminary lemmas,
Section

D.2
gives the proof of
Theorem

17
,
and
Section

D.3
gives the proof of
Theorem

18
.

D.1
Preliminary Lemmas: Entrywise Residual Bounds and Leading-Order Approximations

This section presents several lemmas needed en route to the proof of
Theorem

17
, whose proofs are deferred to
Section

D.4
.
The following lemma shows that the effect of the projection matrices on the random noise tensor
\mathcal{Z}
is sufficiently small.

Lemma 8
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

17
,
the following bounds hold with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
:

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}\mu_{0}^{3}\frac{r^{3/2}}{p^{3/2}};

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}};

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}};

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

The next lemma shows that the terms involving
\mathcal{T}
and at least two differences of projection matrices is sufficiently small.

Lemma 9
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

17
, the following bounds hold with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
:

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\kappa\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\kappa\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

Finally, the following lemma shows that the leading-order term
\xi_{ijk}/s_{ijk}
(defined below) is approximately Gaussian.

Lemma 10
.

Assume the conditions of
Theorem

17
hold. Define

\displaystyle\xi_{ijk}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}+e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}+e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}.

Then it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}\leq t\bigg\}-\Phi(t)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{C_{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}.

D.2
Proof of
Theorem

17

With these lemmas in hand, we are now prepared to prove
Theorem

17
.

Proof of
Theorem

17
.

First, note that

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\big(\mathcal{T+Z}\big)\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}.

We consider each term separately. First, we will show that the second term is a residual term. Observe that

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{Z}

\displaystyle\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}
