\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}.

Each term consists of terms containing either
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}
or the difference
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}
. Therefore, without loss of generality, since
r_{k}\asymp r
and
p_{k}\asymp p
, it suffices to analyze the following four terms:

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk};

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle(III)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk};

\displaystyle(IV)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}.

Each of these terms are analyzed in
Lemma

8
. Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3})^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\mu_{0}^{3}\sqrt{\log(p)}\frac{r^{3/2}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

We now focus on the term containing
\mathcal{T}
. The strategy will be similar, only now appealing to the distributional characterization for the projections in
Theorem

15
. We note that

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}.

The final term is simply
\mathcal{T}_{ijk}
. Similar to the previous case, the terms appearing all appear with either
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}
or the difference
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}
. We will show that terms with at least two projection-norm differences are small-order terms. Again, since
r_{k}\asymp r
and
p_{k}\asymp p
, it suffices to analyze the two terms

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk};

\displaystyle(II)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}.

By
Lemma

9
, it holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
that

\displaystyle(I)+(II)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\kappa\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

By symmetry, we have shown so far that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\sigma\mu_{0}^{3}\sqrt{\log(p)}\frac{r^{3/2}}{p^{3/2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\kappa\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

We will now argue that the difference terms consist of another leading-order term. More specifically, considering
k=1
, we will show that

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}+o(s_{ijk}).

The other indices will follow by symmetry.

By
Theorem

15
on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{\lx@cref{creftype~refnum}{thm:eigenvectornormality}}}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}

\displaystyle(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{U}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{\Phi}^{(1)})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{U}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\Phi}^{(1)})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}.

The first term satisfies

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{M}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{i,(j-1)p_{3}+k}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathcal{M}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{T}\times_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top})\bigg)({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}.

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{VeryGood}}
it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{r}.

Therefore, on this event,

\displaystyle|e_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\|e_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r^{3/2}}{p^{3/2}}.

In addition,

\displaystyle|\bigg(\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\times_{1}(\mathbf{\Phi}^{(1)})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}|

\displaystyle=|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{(1)}\mathbf{T}_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\Phi}^{(1)}\|\|\mathbf{T}_{1}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\Phi}^{(1)}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{3}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\log(p)r^{2}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\sigma}{p^{3/2}}

The remaining term satisfies

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\times_{1}(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}.

Therefore, we have shown that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
,

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\kappa^{3}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\log(p)r^{2}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

By symmetry among indices, it holds that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{ijk}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}+e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}+e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\log(p)r^{2}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\sigma}{p^{3/2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{3}\sqrt{\log(p)}r^{3/2}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\kappa\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}+e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}+e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}\bigg)+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

(8)

This establishes the leading-order expansion.

Therefore, defining
\xi_{ijk}
as in
Lemma

10
, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{T}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}+\frac{C_{1}}{s_{ijk}}\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{C_{2}}{s_{ijk}}\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{\xi_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}+\frac{\mathrm{err}}{s_{ijk}},

where
C_{1}
and
C_{2}
are some universal constants. Therefore, for any
t\in\mathbb{R}
, by
Lemma

10
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\frac{\mathcal{T}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}\leq t\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\frac{\xi_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}\leq t+\frac{\mathrm{err}}{s_{ijk}}\Bigg\}+C_{3}p^{-9}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi\Bigg\{t+\frac{\mathrm{err}}{s_{ijk}}\Bigg\}+C_{3}p^{-9}+\frac{C_{4}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{C_{5}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi(t)+\frac{\mathrm{err}}{s_{ijk}}+C_{3}p^{-9}+\frac{C_{4}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{C_{5}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}

\displaystyle=\Phi(t)+o(1),

where the final result holds since

\displaystyle s_{ijk}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\min}\left(\left\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\mathbf{V}_{2}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\gg\sigma_{\min}\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\},

and the fact that
\sigma/\sigma_{\min}=O(1)
. By applying the same argument to the other direction, the proof is complete.
∎

D.3
Proof of
Theorem

18

Proof of
Theorem

18
.

We start with the leading-order expansion in (
8
) partway through the proof of
Theorem

17
, which demonstrates that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{ijk}

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}+e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}+e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}\bigg)+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

A straightforward Hoeffding inequality argument shows that with high probability,

\displaystyle|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sigma\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}.

The same bound holds for the other two terms; moreover, this bound is uniform in
i,j
, and
k
. Consequently, by taking a union bound over all
O(p^{3})
entries, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}-\mathcal{T}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sigma\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}},

where the final bound is due to the condition
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
. This holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
. The “consequently” part is immediate.
∎

D.4
Proofs of Preliminary Lemmas from
Section

D.1

In this section we prove the preliminary lemmas from
Section

D.1
.

D.4.1
Proof of
Lemma

8

Proof of
Lemma

8
.

The first bound follows by noting that

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\sum_{abc}\mathcal{Z}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})_{ia}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})_{kc},

which is a linear combination of Subgaussian random variables with Orlicz norm of coefficients bounded by

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}\sum_{a,b,c}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})_{ia}^{2}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})_{jb}^{2}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})_{kc}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}\|e_{i}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|^{2}\|e_{j}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\|^{2}\|e_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{6}\frac{r^{3}}{p^{3}}.

Consequently, Hoeffding’s inequality shows that this term is bounded by
C\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}\mu_{0}^{3}\frac{r^{3/2}}{p^{3/2}}
with probability at least
1-O(p^{-10})
.

Next, observe that
Theorem

15
implies that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

In addition, under the conditions of
Theorem

17
, it holds that

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}

which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda/\sigma}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}.

Hence, with probability
1-O(p^{-9})
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}.

(9)

Similar bounds hold for the other modes as well. Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{abc}\mathcal{Z}_{abc}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})_{ia}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})_{kc}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{p_{1}}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\max_{a}\bigg|\sum_{bc}\mathcal{Z}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})_{kc}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{pr\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg)\max_{a}\bigg|\sum_{bc}\mathcal{Z}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})_{kc}\bigg|

Hoeffding’s inequality and a union bound reveals that

\displaystyle\max_{a}\bigg|\sum_{bc}\mathcal{Z}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})_{kc}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\sqrt{\log(p)}

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{pr\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

For the next term, we note that

\displaystyle\bigg|

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{i,(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\|\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{i\cdot}\|\big\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{\cdot,(j-1)p_{3}+k}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\big\|\bigg(\mathbf{Z}_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{\cdot,(j-1)p_{3}+k}\big\|.

Define the matrix
\mathbf{A}^{(2)}(j)
as the
p_{2}\times p_{2}
matrix whose rows are all zero except for the
j
’th row, which is equal to the
j
’th row of
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}
, and define
\mathbf{A}^{(3)}(k)
as the
p_{3}\times p_{3}
matrix whose rows are all zero except for the
k
’th row, which is equal to the
k
’th row of
{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}
. Observe that since both
\mathbf{A}^{(2)}(j)
and
\mathbf{A}^{(3)}(k)
are rank at most
2r
, it holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}(\mathbf{A}^{(2)}(j)\otimes\mathbf{A}^{(3)}(k))\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau_{k}\|\mathbf{A}^{(2)}(j)\|\|\mathbf{A}^{(3)}(k)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{pr}\|\|\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{j\cdot}\|({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})_{k\cdot}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{pr}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{pr}\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda},

where we have implicitly used the bound in Equation (
9
). Putting it together, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}},

since
\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\sqrt{pr}
.

By a similar argument, it is straightforward to show that with this same probability,

\displaystyle\bigg|

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathcal{Z}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{pr}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{pr}\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

Aggregating these bounds completes the proof.
∎

D.4.2
Proof of
Lemma

9

Proof of
Lemma

9
.

The proof is similar to
Lemma

8
. Again, observe that
Theorem

15
and the conditions of
Theorem

17
implies that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
,

\displaystyle\max_{k}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}.

Therefore, we note that on this event,

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(\mathcal{T}

\displaystyle\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}({\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|e_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{3}\bigg((\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\otimes(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\bigg)e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathbf{U}}_{3}\|_{2,\infty}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{3}\|\bigg\|\bigg((\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\otimes(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\bigg)\bigg\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{1}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{3}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\kappa\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

Similarly, also on this event,

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg(

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}\times_{1}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\times_{2}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top})\times_{3}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top})\bigg)_{ijk}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{T}_{1}\bigg[\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\bigg)\otimes\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg]e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{T}_{1}\|\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\kappa\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\kappa\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}},

since
\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\sqrt{p\log(p)}
. This completes the proof.
∎

D.4.3
Proof of
Lemma

10

Proof of
Lemma

10
.

We first observe that the random variable

\displaystyle\xi_{ijk}

\displaystyle\coloneqq e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}+e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}+e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}

is a linear combination of random variables belonging to
\mathcal{Z}
. Note that
\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})
may not equal
s^{2}_{ijk}
. First, we will show that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})

\displaystyle=s^{2}_{ijk}+o(s^{2}_{ijk}).

Next, we will calculate the moment bounds needed to apply the Berry-Esseen Theorem, and finally we will put it all together.

•

Step 1: Variance Calculation:
Since
\xi_{ijk}
is a sum of three separate terms, we will first calculate the contribution of the cross terms to the variance of
\xi_{ijk}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[e_{i}^{\top}

\displaystyle\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg]\bigg[e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}\sum_{(l_{1},l_{2})\in\Omega}|e_{l_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}|\big|e_{l_{2}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\big|,

where the sum is over the set
\Omega
containing indices
(l_{1},l_{2})
such that
(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{il_{1}}=(\mathbf{Z}_{2})_{kl_{2}}
(i.e., the indices corresponding to the same elements of the underlying tensor
\mathcal{Z}
). We note that the general formula is given by

\displaystyle(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{i,(j-1)p_{3}+b}=(\mathbf{Z}_{2})_{j,(b-1)p_{1}+i},

which shows that the two terms have
p_{3}\lesssim p
terms in common (since
1\leq b\leq p_{3}
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{(l_{1},l_{2})\in\Omega}|e_{l_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}|\big|e_{l_{2}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim p\max_{l_{1},l_{2}}|e_{l_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}|\big|e_{l_{2}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\big|

\displaystyle\leq p\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\|\mathbf{V}_{2}\|_{2,\infty}\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq p\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{2p}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}\bigg),

where we have used the inequality
2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}
. Therefore, by symmetry,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}

\displaystyle\bigg(e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}+e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}+e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Var}\bigg(e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg)+\mathrm{Var}\bigg(e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{Var}\bigg(e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg)\bigg).

To calculate the remaining terms, we simply note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg(e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{p_{2}p_{3}}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{il}^{2}(e_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{p_{2}p_{3}}\sigma^{2}_{il}(e_{l}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k})^{2}

\displaystyle=\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(i)}_{1}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2},

where we recall that
\Sigma^{(i)}_{1}
is the diagonal matrix whose entries are the variances
\sigma^{2}_{il}
. Consequently,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\bigg(

\displaystyle e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg)+\mathrm{Var}\bigg(e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{Var}\bigg(e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(i)}_{1}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{2}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(k)}_{3}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=s_{ijk}^{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})

\displaystyle=s_{ijk}^{2}+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg)\bigg),

\displaystyle=s^{2}_{ijk}+o(s^{2}_{ijk}),

where the final inequality holds since

\displaystyle s^{2}_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(i)}_{1}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{2}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(k)}_{3}\big)^{1/2}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\min}^{2}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\gg\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg)\bigg),

since
\sigma/\sigma_{\min}=O(1)
and
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim\sqrt{p}
. Consequently,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}{s^{2}_{ijk}}

\displaystyle=1+O\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}\bigg)

(10)

which will be useful later on.

•

Step 2: Third Moment Calculation
:
In order to apply the Berry-Esseen Theorem, we will a bound on the third absolute moment of the sum of the independent random variables in question. To avoid complicated notation, let
(b,c)
be the index of the first matricization corresponding to its
(i,b,c)
entry, and similarly for
(a,c)
and
(a,b)
(with second and third matricization and
j
and
k
replaced respectively). We can then write

\displaystyle\xi_{ijk}

\displaystyle=\sum_{b=1}^{p_{2}}\sum_{c=1}^{p_{3}}\mathcal{Z}_{ibc}\bigg[(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}+\mathbb{I}_{\{b=j\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{c=k\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{a\neq i,a=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{c=1}^{p_{3}}\mathcal{Z}_{ajc}\bigg[\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}+\mathbb{I}_{\{c=k\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{a\neq i,a=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{b\neq j,b=1}^{p_{2}}\mathcal{Z}_{abk}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j},

which, when written in this form, is precisely a sum of independent random variables. There are
O(p^{2})
many terms in this sum. We will need to bound

\displaystyle\sum_{b=1}^{p_{2}}

\displaystyle\sum_{c=1}^{p_{3}}\mathbb{E}\bigg|\mathcal{Z}_{ibc}\bigg[(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}+\mathbb{I}_{\{b=j\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}

(11)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{c=k\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg]\bigg|^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{a\neq i,a=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{c=1}^{p_{3}}\mathbb{E}\bigg|\mathcal{Z}_{ajc}\bigg[\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}+\mathbb{I}_{\{c=k\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg]\bigg|^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{a\neq i,a=1}^{p_{1}}\sum_{b\neq j,b=1}^{p_{2}}\mathbb{E}\bigg|\mathcal{Z}_{abk}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg|^{3}.

(12)

By subgaussianity (e.g.,
Vershynin (2018)
, Proposition 2.5.2), it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg|

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{ibc}\bigg[(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}+\mathbb{I}_{\{b=j\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{c=k\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg]\bigg|^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}+\mathbb{I}_{\{b=j\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{c=k\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg|^{3}\mathbb{E}|\mathcal{Z}_{ibc}|^{3}

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma^{3}\bigg|(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}+\mathbb{I}_{\{b=j\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{c=k\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg|^{3}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\sigma^{3}\bigg(\bigg|(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg|^{3}+\bigg|\mathbb{I}_{\{b=j\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}\bigg|^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\mathbb{I}_{\{c=k\}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg|^{3}\bigg)

Substituting this bound into
Eq.

12
and rearranging yields the upper bound

\displaystyle C^{\prime}

\displaystyle\sigma^{3}\Bigg[\sum_{b,c}\big|(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}\big|^{3}+\sum_{a,c}\big|\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}\big|^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{a,b}\big|\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\big|^{3}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\sigma^{3}\bigg[\max_{b,c}\big|(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}\big|\sum_{b,c}\big|(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{(b,c),(j-1)p_{3}+k}\big|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\max_{a,c}\big|\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}\big|\sum_{a,c}\big|\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{(a,c),(k-1)p_{1}+i}\big|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\max_{a,b}\big|\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\big|\sum_{a,b}\big|\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{(a,b),(i-1)p_{2}+j}\big|^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{3}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}\bigg(\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\|^{2}+\|\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\|^{2}+\|\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\|^{2}\bigg)

(13)

In addition, we note that from the previous step it holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})

\displaystyle=s_{ijk}^{2}+C\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}\bigg(\left\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\right\|^{2}\bigg).

This implies that there is some constant
c>0
such that

\displaystyle\mathrm{{\rm var}}(\xi_{ijk})^{3/2}

\displaystyle\geq cs_{ijk}^{3}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle s_{ijk}^{3}\geq\sigma_{\min}^{3}\bigg(\left\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\right\|^{2}\bigg)^{3/2}.

Therefore, dividing (
13
) by the
\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})^{3/2}
yields that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})^{3/2}}\sigma^{3}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}\bigg(\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\|^{2}+\|\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\|^{2}+\|\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{cs_{ijk}^{3}}\sigma^{3}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}\bigg(\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\|^{2}+\|\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}\|^{2}+\|\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{3}}{\sigma_{\min}^{3}}\ \bigg(\left\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\right\|^{2}\bigg)^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}\bigg(\left\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\right\|^{2}\bigg)^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}\frac{p^{3/2}}{\kappa\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}},

where we have used the condition

\displaystyle\bigg(\left\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\right\|^{2}+\left\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\mathbf{V}_{3}\right\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}\gg\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}.

•

Step 3: Putting It All Together
:
By the Berry-Esseen Theorem, for any
t\in\mathbb{R}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}\leq t\bigg\}-\Phi(t)\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi_{ijk}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}\leq t\frac{s_{ijk}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}\bigg\}-\Phi(t)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi_{ijk}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}\leq t\frac{s_{ijk}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}\bigg\}-\Phi\bigg(t\frac{s_{ijk}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\Phi(t)-\Phi\bigg(t\frac{s_{ijk}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\bigg|\Phi(t)-\Phi\bigg(t\frac{s_{ijk}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}\bigg)\bigg|.

Note that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\bigg|\Phi(t)-\ \Phi\bigg(t\frac{s_{ijk}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\mathcal{A}\ \mathrm{measurable}}\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg(Z\in\mathcal{A}\bigg)-\mathbb{P}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}{s_{ijk}}Z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle=D_{TV}\bigg(Z,\frac{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}{s_{ijk}}Z^{\prime}\bigg)

where
Z
and
Z^{\prime}
are independent standard Gaussians and
D_{TV}
is the total variation distance. By Theorem 1.3 of
Devroye et al. (2022)
, it holds that

\displaystyle D_{TV}\bigg(Z,\frac{\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}}{s_{ijk}}Z^{\prime}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{3}\bigg(1-\frac{\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})}{s^{2}_{ijk}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O(\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}),

where we have used (
10
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi_{ijk}}{s_{ijk}}\leq t\bigg\}-\Phi(t)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{C_{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}.

This completes the proof.

∎

Appendix E
Proof of Validity of Confidence Intervals (Theorem
19
and Theorem
20
)

In this section we prove the validity of our confidence regions (
Theorem

19
) and intervals (
Theorem

20
). In
Section

E.1
we state several preliminary lemmas needed to guarantee good approximation of our plug-in estimates, and their proofs are in
Section

E.4
. In
Section

E.3
we prove
Theorem

20
, and in

Section

E.2
we prove
Theorem

19
.

E.1
Preliminary Lemmas: Plug-In Estimate Proximity

First we show that our estimates
\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}
from
Algorithm

3
and
Algorithm

4
are sufficiently close to
\mathbf{V}_{k}
and
\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}
with respect to both
\|\cdot\|
and
\|\cdot\|_{2,\infty}
. In what follows, recall that
\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}
are defined as the leading
r_{k}
right singular vectors and singular values of the matrix
\mathcal{M}_{k}(\mathcal{\widetilde{T}})\bigg(\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{\top}\big)\otimes\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{\top}\big)\bigg)
.

Lemma 11
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

14
, and suppose that

\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}.

Let
\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}
be as in
Algorithm

3
and
Algorithm

4
. Let
\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}\coloneqq\mathrm{sgn}(\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k})
. Then with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
,

\displaystyle\max_{k}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k},\mathbf{V}_{k})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda};

\displaystyle\max_{k}\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}-\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}};

\displaystyle\max_{k}\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg).

Next, in order to prove
Theorem

19
, we will need the following concentration inequality for the estimated matrix
\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}
versus the true matrix
\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}
.

Lemma 12
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

19
, and define
\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}
as in
Algorithm

3
. Then with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p}\bigg),

where
\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}=\mathrm{sgn}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k})
.

E.2
Proof of
Theorem

19

We now prove
Theorem

19
.

Proof of
Theorem

19
.

Here we suppress the dependence on
t
.
Lemma

12
reveals that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma}{\lambda}\times o(1)

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
. Snce
\mathbf{\Gamma}_{k}^{(m)}
has smallest eigenvalue at most
c\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda}
(see the proof of
Lemma

12
), by Weyl’s inequality it therefore holds that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\bigg(\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\bigg)\gtrsim\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\lambda}.

(14)

Hence, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
it holds that

\displaystyle\|e_{m}^{\top}\bigg(

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\bigg)\big((\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}-(\mathbf{W}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top})^{-1/2}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{m}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\big)\|\|(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}-(\mathbf{W}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top})^{-1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{m}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}\big)\|\|(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}-(\mathbf{W}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top})^{-1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}\|(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{-1/2}\bigg((\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{1/2}-\mathbf{W}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k})^{1/2}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)(\mathbf{W}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top})^{-1/2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k})}\|(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{1/2}-\mathbf{W}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k})^{1/2}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\|(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})^{1/2}-\mathbf{W}_{k}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k})^{1/2}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma\kappa\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}\log^{3/2}(p)+\sigma\kappa^{3}r\mu_{0}\sqrt{p}\log^{2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r\log^{3/2}(p)}{p}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\log(p)}{p}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\varepsilon,

where in the fourth line we have implicitly used
Theorem

21
. For a convex set
A
, we denote
A^{\varepsilon}
as the
\varepsilon
-enlargement via

\displaystyle A^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\{x:d(x,A)\leq\varepsilon\}.

By Theorem 1.2 of
Raic\v (2019)
, if
Z
is an isotropic
\mathbb{R}^{r_{k}}
dimensional random vector, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg(Z\in A^{\varepsilon}\setminus A\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{1/4}\varepsilon.

The proof is now straightforward. Define
A_{\alpha}
as the confidence region such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(Z\in A_{\alpha})=1-\alpha,

where
Z\sim N(0,I_{r_{k}})
. Then by
Theorem

16
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{e_{m}^{\top}

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\in\mathrm{C.I.}_{\alpha}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k})\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{e_{m}^{\top}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\bigg)\in\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}A_{\alpha}\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{e_{m}^{\top}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\in\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}A_{\alpha}\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\Bigg\{\bigg\{e_{m}^{\top}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\in\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}A_{\alpha}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\bigcap\bigg\{\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg(\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}-\mathbf{W}_{k}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg\|\leq\varepsilon\bigg\}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg\|e_{m}^{\top}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg(\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}-\mathbf{W}_{k}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}\mathbf{W}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg\|>\varepsilon\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{e_{m}^{\top}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{k}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\in\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}A_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+p^{-6}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{2}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}+p^{-6}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg(Z\in A_{\alpha}\bigg)-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}\setminus A\bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{2}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}+p^{-6}

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{1/4}\varepsilon++\frac{\mu_{0}r^{2}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}+p^{-6}

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{1/2}\varepsilon++\frac{\mu_{0}r^{2}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}+p^{-6}

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}\log^{3/2}(p)+\sigma\kappa^{3}r\mu_{0}\sqrt{p}\log^{2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r\log^{3/2}(p)}{p}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{2}\log(p)}{p}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{2}}{p}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)r^{3/2}\sqrt{p}}{\lambda}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\kappa}{\sqrt{p}}+p^{-6}

\displaystyle=o(1),

where the term is
o(1)
as long as

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gg\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{2}\log^{2}(p)r^{2}\sqrt{p},

which is true by assumption, and

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log^{3/2}(p)+\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{5/2}\log(p)\ll p

which holds as long as
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
, which is also by assumption. This completes the proof.
∎

E.3
Proof of
Theorem

20

Proof of
Theorem

20
.

We will model the argument in the proof of Theorem 4.11 of
Chen et al. (2021)
, where we will argue that
\widehat{s}^{2}_{ijk}-s_{ijk}^{2}
is sufficiently small. We first introduce an auxiliary term

\displaystyle\widetilde{s}^{2}_{ijk}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sum_{a}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big)_{ia}^{2}\big(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}+\sum_{b}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}_{jb}\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{b,(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{c}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{3}\big)^{2}_{kc}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{c,(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{2}.

We will compare both
\widehat{s}^{2}_{ijk}
and
s_{ijk}^{2}
to
\widetilde{s}^{2}_{ijk}
.

•

Step 1: Showing
\widehat{s}^{2}_{ijk}\approx\widetilde{s}^{2}_{ijk}
:

Observe that

\displaystyle|\widehat{s}^{2}_{ijk}-\widetilde{s}^{2}_{ijk}|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\sum_{a}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big)_{ia}^{2}\big(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}-\big(\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}\big)_{ia}^{2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\sum_{b}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{2}\big)^{2}_{jb}\big(\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{b,(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{2}-\big(\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{2}\big)^{2}_{jb}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{2}^{\top}\big)_{b,(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\sum_{c}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{3}\big)^{2}_{kc}\big(\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{c,(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{2}-\big(\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{3}\big)^{2}_{kc}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{3}^{\top}\big)_{c,(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{2}\bigg|.

We will focus on the first term, since the other terms will follow by symmetry. Note that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{a}

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big)_{ia}^{2}\big(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}-\big(\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}\big)_{ia}^{2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{a}\big|(\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1})_{ia}^{2}-(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}\big|\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}

(15)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{a}(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}\bigg|\big(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big)^{2}_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}-\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\big)^{2}_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{a}|(\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1})_{ia}^{2}-(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}|\sum_{a}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\big)_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\max_{a}|(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}|p^{2}\max_{a}\bigg|\big(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big)^{2}_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}-\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\big)^{2}_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\|\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}\|_{\max}+\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\|_{\max}\bigg)\|\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\|_{\max}\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+p^{2}\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\|_{\max}^{2}\bigg(\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}+\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}\bigg)\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}.

(16)

It is straightforward to note that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle=\max_{a,b,c}|\mathcal{Z}_{abc}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}.

In addition, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle=\max_{i,j}|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}}\|_{2,\infty}+\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\bigg)^{2}.

We note that by
Lemma

11
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}r\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}

\displaystyle\leq\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}

provided that

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}r\sqrt{p\log(p)}.

(17)

Note that we require that

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}.

In addition,
Theorem

21
requires that
\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\kappa p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
. Therefore, together these imply that

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma

\displaystyle\gtrsim\kappa p^{1/4}\sqrt{p\log(p)}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\kappa^{3}r^{3/2}\mu_{0}^{2}\log(p)\sqrt{p}

\displaystyle\gg\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}r\sqrt{p\log(p)},

so that
Eq.

17
holds. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}.

Therefore, the bound in (
16
) reduces to

\displaystyle\bigg(\|\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}\|_{\max}+\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg)\|\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\|_{\max}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}+\sigma^{2}\log(p)\mu_{0}^{2}r\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}.

(18)

We now note that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{\widehat{Z}}-\mathcal{Z}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{T+Z-\widehat{T}-Z}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{T-\widehat{T}}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}},

which holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
, which holds by
Theorem

18
. As a byproduct, we also obtain that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{1}-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\|_{\max}+\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}

provided that

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\mu_{0}^{2}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)},

which is guaranteed by the conditions in
Theorem

21
as well as the assumption
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
. Finally, by
Lemma

11
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}}^{\top})\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}+\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}}^{\top})\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{3}\kappa^{2}r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda p^{3/2}}.

Therefore, plugging in these estimates to (
18
), we see that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
,

\displaystyle|\widehat{s}^{2}_{ijk}-\widetilde{s}_{ijk}^{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg(\frac{\mu_{0}\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\log(p)\mu_{0}^{2}r\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{3}\kappa^{2}r^{2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda p^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{6}\kappa^{3}r^{4}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{5/2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}.

(19)

•

Step 2: Showing
\widetilde{s}^{2}_{ijk}\approx s^{2}_{ijk}
:
Note that the term
\widetilde{s}^{2}_{ijk}
is a sum of independent subexponential random variables, so we will apply Bernstein’s inequality to it. In order to avoid additional cross-term covariance factors, we will apply it to each of the three separate terms.

Define

\displaystyle(s^{(i)})^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(i)}_{1}\big)^{1/2}\right\|^{2};

\displaystyle(s^{(j)})^{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(j)}_{2}\big)^{1/2}\right\|^{2};

\displaystyle(s^{(k)})^{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(k)}_{3}\big)^{1/2}\right\|^{2},

so that
s^{2}_{ijk}=(s^{(i)})^{2}+(s^{(j)})^{2}+(s^{(k)})^{2}.
Define
(\widetilde{s}^{(i)})^{2},(\widetilde{s}^{(j)})^{2},
and
(\widetilde{s}^{(k)})^{2}
similarly. Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|\widetilde{s}^{2}_{ijk}-s^{2}_{ijk}|\geq t\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 3\max_{c\in ijk}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|(s^{(c)})^{2}-(\widetilde{s}^{(c)})^{2}|\geq t/3\bigg\}.

Without loss of generality, we focus on the first term; i.e.,
s^{(i)}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(\widetilde{s}^{(i)})^{2}=(s^{(i)})^{2},

so that the difference
(\widetilde{s}^{(i)})^{2}-(s^{(i)})^{2}
term is a sum of mean-zero random variables. In order to apply Bernstein’s inequality (Theorem 2.8.1 in
Vershynin (2018)
), we need to bound:

\displaystyle A_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\sum_{a}\|((\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}-\sigma^{2}_{ia})(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}^{2};

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\max_{l}\|((\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}-\sigma^{2}_{ia})(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}.

However,

\displaystyle\sum_{a}\|

\displaystyle((\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}-\sigma^{2}_{ia})(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{a}(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{4}\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}-\sigma^{2}_{ia}\|_{\psi_{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{4}\max_{a}(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}\sum_{a}(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{4}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}\|(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{\cdot,(j-1)p_{3}+k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{4},

and

\displaystyle\max_{a}\|

\displaystyle((\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}-\sigma^{2}_{ia})(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{a}|(\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})_{a,(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{2}|\|(\mathbf{Z}_{1})_{ia}^{2}-\sigma^{2}_{ia}\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\|\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}.

Therefore, by Bernstein’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle|(\widetilde{s}^{(i)})^{2}-(s^{(i)})^{2}|\geq Ct\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-c\min\bigg(\frac{t^{2}}{\sigma^{4}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{4}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}},\frac{t}{\sigma^{2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p^{2}}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}}\bigg)\bigg\}.

Taking

\displaystyle t

\displaystyle=C\sigma^{2}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p},

yields that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|(\widetilde{s}^{(i)})^{2}-(s^{(i)})^{2}|

\displaystyle\geq C\sigma^{2}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg\{-c\min\bigg(C^{2}\log(p),C\frac{p\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}}\bigg)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq Cp^{-20},

since
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim\sqrt{p}
. Consequently, by symmetry and the union bound, we obtain with this same probability that

\displaystyle|\widetilde{s}_{ijk}^{2}-s_{ijk}^{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\ll s^{2}_{ijk},

since
s^{2}_{ijk}
satisfies the lower bound

\displaystyle s^{2}_{ijk}\geq\sigma_{\min}^{2}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg),

and
\sigma/\sigma_{\min}=O(1)
by assumption.

•

Step 3: Combining These Bounds
:
Combining steps 1 and 2, we see that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
that

\displaystyle|s^{2}_{ijk}-\widehat{s}^{2}_{ijk}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}+\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}},

where we used the bound (
19
). Therefore,

\displaystyle|s^{2}_{ijk}-\widehat{s}^{2}_{ijk}|

\displaystyle\ll s^{2}_{ijk}

under the assumption that

\displaystyle s^{2}_{ijk}\gg\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg\}.

(20)

The second term is guaranteed by the assumption in
Theorem

20
. For the first term, recall that the assumption in
Theorem

17
implies that

\displaystyle s_{ijk}^{2}

\displaystyle\gg\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{4}\mu_{0}^{6}r^{4}\log(p)}{p^{3}},

so that the assumption in (
20
) is met.

From this expansion, it holds that

\displaystyle\widehat{s}_{ijk}

\displaystyle=s_{ijk}(1+o(1))

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
. Therefore, by
Theorem

17
, we have that

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{ijk}\in\mathrm{C.I.}_{\alpha}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk})\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{ijk}|\leq z_{\alpha/2}\sqrt{\widehat{s}^{2}_{ijk}}\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{ijk}|\leq z_{\alpha/2}s_{ijk}(1+o(1))\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\Phi\big((1+o(1)\big)z_{\alpha/2}-\Phi\big(-(1+o(1))z_{\alpha/2}\big)-(1-\alpha)\bigg|+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\Phi(z_{\alpha/2})-\Phi(-z_{\alpha/2})-(1-\alpha)\bigg|+2\bigg|\Phi\big((1+o(1))z_{\alpha/2}\big)-\Phi(z_{\alpha/2})\bigg|+o(1)

\displaystyle\leq o(1),

which follows by the Lipschitz continuity of
\Phi
. This completes the proof.

∎

E.4
Proof of Preliminary Lemmas from
Section

E.1

This section contains all the proofs from
Section

E.1
.

E.4.1
Proof of
Lemma

11

Proof of
Lemma

11
.

Without loss of generality, we consider
k=1
, and recall we assume
t
is such that
t=t_{0}+1
, where
t_{0}
is such that
Theorem

21
holds. We note that
\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}
is the right orthonormal matrix in the truncated SVD of the matrix

\displaystyle\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg)\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\bigg).

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}^{\top}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbf{T}_{1}-\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{1}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg)\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{1}-\mathbf{T}_{1}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{T}_{1}\bigg(\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}-\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\bigg).

Taking norms, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{E}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{T}_{1}\|\bigg\|\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\otimes\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}-\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\bigg)\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}\big)^{\top}\bigg)\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{1}\max\bigg\{\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{2})\|,\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)},{\mathbf{U}}_{3})\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+C\sigma\sqrt{pr}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}+\sigma\sqrt{pr}

(21)

\displaystyle\ll\lambda,

which holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})

by (
5
)
. Here we have used the fact that
t_{0}+1\leq t\leq t_{\max}
, as well as the bounds on the term
\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\|\leq C\sigma\sqrt{pr}
, which holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
(defined in
Section

B.2
). Therefore, by the Davis-Kahan Theorem, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta\big(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1},\mathbf{V}_{1}\big)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}+\sigma\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda},

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
. The same argument goes through for the other modes as well.

We now consider the
\ell_{2,\infty}
error for
\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}
. We will apply Theorem 3.7 of
Cape et al. (2019)
, (with
\mathbf{X}
therein defined as
\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}
and
{\mathbf{U}}
and
\mathbf{V}
switched from their notation) to see that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2\bigg(\frac{\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+2\bigg(\frac{\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\|_{2,\infty}}{\lambda}\bigg)\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|

\displaystyle\quad+2\bigg(\frac{\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\|_{2,\infty}}{\lambda}\bigg)\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1},\mathbf{V}_{1})\|^{2}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2\bigg(\frac{\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+2\bigg(\frac{\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\|_{2,\infty}}{\lambda}\bigg)\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{1}^{(t)},{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1},\mathbf{V}_{1})\|^{2}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty},

(22)

\displaystyle\coloneqq(I)+(II)+(III),

(23)

where we have used the fact that
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}=0
. Therefore, it suffices to bound each of the other three terms above.

•

The term
(I)
: We note that numerator satisfies

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\bigg[\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\big(\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}+\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\big)-\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\bigg]{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}
