\displaystyle\quad+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{T}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\|\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\|,

where the final inequality is due to the fact that
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{(t-1)})^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{(t-1)})^{\top}
is an orthogonal projection matrix and hence equals its square.
From the proof of
Lemma

8
, under the condition
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
,
Theorem

15
implies that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
,

\displaystyle\max_{k}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\big)\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}-{\mathbf{U}}_{2}{\mathbf{U}}_{2}^{\top}\big)\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3}{\mathbf{U}}_{3}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\sqrt{\frac{r}{p}}\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

In addition, with this same probability,
by (
5
)
it holds that

\displaystyle\max_{k}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{k}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda/\sigma}.

Plugging in all these bounds yields

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{I}-

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}+\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\kappa\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg)\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda_{1}\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}+\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\kappa\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\quad+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)\mathbf{Z}_{1}^{\top}\|.

On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Z}_{1}\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{2}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{3}^{\top}\bigg)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{pr};

Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
,

\displaystyle\frac{\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda}\bigg\{\lambda_{1}\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}+\lambda_{1}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\kappa\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}+\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\sigma\sqrt{pr}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r\sqrt{\log(p)}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}+\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}

(24)

•

The Term
(II)
:
We note that the numerator of the second term in (
23
) is of the form

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}(\mathbf{I}-{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top})\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top})\mathbf{E}\|.

By repeating the argument above, this term satisfies the same upper bound as
(I)
as the
\sin\Theta
distance is upper bounded by one.

•

The Term
(III)
:
The only remaining term is the term

\displaystyle\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1},\mathbf{V}_{1})\|^{2}\|\mathbf{V}_{1}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\|\mathbf{E}\|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}

by the Davis-Kahan Theorem. We have already showed in (
21
) that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{E}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\|\mathbf{E}\|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\big(\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}\big)^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\sigma^{2}pr+\kappa^{4}\sigma^{2}p\log(p)+\sigma^{2}\kappa^{2}p\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r}\big(r+\kappa^{4}\log(p)+\kappa^{2}\sqrt{r\log(p)}\big)}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mu_{0}\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\kappa^{4}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}}.

This bound is smaller than the bound in (
24
) as long as

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\mu_{0}}\sqrt{p\log(p)}.

Recall that we assume that
\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\kappa p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
and that
\kappa\lesssim p^{1/4}
. Therefore, (
24
) dominates this upper bound.

Putting these bounds together, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}-\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{1}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\lambda\sqrt{p}},

which proves the second assertion.

Next, we show that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg)

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
.
Since
\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}
are the leading
r_{k}
singular values of the matrix

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{T}_{k}+\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+1}^{\top}\otimes\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k+2}^{\top}\big),

then it holds that

\displaystyle(\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k})_{r_{k}}

\displaystyle\geq(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k})_{r_{k}}-2\|\mathbf{E}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\gtrsim(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k})_{r_{k}},

where
\mathbf{E}_{k}
is as in the previous part of the lemma (where above we suppressed the dependence of
\mathbf{E}_{k}
on
k
). Hence
\|\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\|\lesssim\lambda^{-1}
, which will be useful in the sequel.

Observe that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}(\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k})\|+\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}-\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|\big(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}-\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\|+\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}-\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\|}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\|+\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}-\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\|}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\|}{\lambda^{2}}.

Note that since
\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}=\mathrm{sgn}({\mathbf{U}}_{k},\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k})
, it holds on the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{Good}}

by
Eq.

5
and the same argument as (
7
)
that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\kappa\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg)^{2};

and similarly that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}-\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{V}_{k},\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}+\sigma\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\bigg)^{2},

which holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
by the previous part of this proof. For the remaining term, we note that by the eigenvector eigenvalue equation, it holds that
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}=(\mathbf{T}_{k}+\mathbf{E}_{k})\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}
, and hence that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\|

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{T}_{k}+\mathbf{E}_{k}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\|

\displaystyle=\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{E}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)},

where the final inequality holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
by (
21
). Putting these all together shows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}+\sigma\sqrt{pr}}{\lambda}\bigg)^{2}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg),

which completes the proof.
∎

E.4.2
Proof of
Lemma

12

Proof of
Lemma

12
.

First we will show that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p}\bigg)

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
. Define
\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}
as the diagonal matrix whose diagonal entries are the squared entries of
e_{m}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{k}
. We will proceed in steps. First, we note that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\Sigma^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\bigg(\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\Sigma^{(m)}_{k}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\bigg(\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\bigg(\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\Sigma^{(m)}_{k}\bigg)\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\coloneqq\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}.

In the subsequent steps, we bound
\alpha_{1}
,
\alpha_{2}
, and
\alpha_{3}
, but first we obtain several preliminary bounds.

•

Step 1: Initial Bounds:
First, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\|

\displaystyle=\max_{l}|\big(\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}_{k}\big)_{ml}^{2}-\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{ml}^{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\|\mathcal{\widehat{Z}}\|_{\max}+\|\mathcal{Z}\|_{\max}\bigg)\|\mathcal{\widehat{Z}}-\mathcal{Z}\|_{\max}.

By
Theorem

18
, with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{\widehat{Z}-Z}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}-T}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

Since
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
, and
\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\kappa p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
, it also holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{\widehat{Z}}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{\widehat{Z}-Z}\|_{\max}+\|\mathcal{Z}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
. Therefore, with this same probability,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\|\mathcal{\widehat{Z}}\|_{\max}+\|\mathcal{Z}\|_{\max}\bigg)\|\mathcal{\widehat{Z}}-\mathcal{Z}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}-T}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(p)}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

(25)

This argument reveals that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\|+\|\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}+\max_{l}|(\mathbf{Z}_{k})_{ml}^{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}+\sigma^{2}\log(p)

\displaystyle\asymp\sigma^{2}\log(p),

(26)

where the penultimate line is due to the fact that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
,

\displaystyle\max_{l}|\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{ml}^{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}+\max_{l}|\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{ml}^{2}-\sigma^{2}_{ml}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\log(p),

together with the assumption that
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
and
\lambda/\sigma\gtrsim\kappa p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}
, so that the first two terms are less than
\sigma^{2}\log(p)
.

In addition, by
Lemma

11
(whose statement does not depend on this lemma), it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}\bigg)

(27)

with probability
1-O(p^{-9})
. In addition,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}^{\top}-\mathbf{V}_{k}\|

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}-\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}-\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{V}_{k},\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}\|\sin\Theta(\mathbf{V}_{k},\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{pr}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{p\log(p)}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{rp\log(p)}}{\lambda}.

(28)

Finally, by (
27
) and (
28
), we have that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\big(\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}^{\top}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\big)\|+\|\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{W}_{\mathbf{V}_{k}}^{\top}\big)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{rp\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}.

(29)

•

Step 2: Bounding
\alpha_{1}
:
We have that

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\bigg)\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|

(30)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{rp\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

(31)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{3}r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma^{3}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{3}}

(32)

where (
30
) holds by (
25
) and (
31
) follows from (
29
). This bound holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
.

•

Step 3: Bounding
\alpha_{2}
:
Note that

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\|\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\left(\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{k}\widehat{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{\top}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\bigg\|\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\|\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\|}{\lambda}+\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}\|\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

(33)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}\|

(34)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}+\kappa^{2}\sigma\sqrt{rp\log(p)}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg)

(35)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{3}r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma^{3}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}p}

(36)

where (
33
) follows from (
25
), (
34
) follows from (
26
), and (
35
) follows from (
29
). We note that (
36
) follows since

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{3}\sqrt{p}}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{3}},

which holds on the assumption
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
.

•

Step 4: Bounding
\alpha_{3}
:
Finally, we note that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg(\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\Sigma^{(m)}_{k}\bigg)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq r\|\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\bigg(\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\Sigma^{(m)}_{k}\bigg)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{V}_{k}\|_{\max}.

We now note that the
i_{1},i_{2}
entry of the above matrix can be written as

\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}}

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{2}i_{2}}\bigg(\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\Sigma^{(m)}_{k}\bigg)_{j_{1}j_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{2}}\bigg(\widetilde{\Sigma}^{(m)}_{k}-\Sigma^{(m)}_{k}\bigg)_{j_{1}j_{1}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{2}}\bigg(\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{mj_{1}}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{mj_{1}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\eta_{i_{1}i_{2}}.

This is a sum of
p_{-k}
independent subexponential random variables, so we will apply Bernstein’s inequality (Theorem 2.8.1 of
Vershynin (2018)
). We note that

\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1}}\bigg\|\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}

\displaystyle\big)_{j_{1}i_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{2}}\bigg(\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{mj_{1}}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{mj_{1}}^{2}\bigg)\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{1}}^{2}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{2}}^{2}\|\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{mj_{1}}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{mj_{1}}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{4}\max_{j_{1}}\|e_{j_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\|^{2}\sum_{j_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{4}}{\lambda^{4}}\|\mathbf{V}_{k}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\sum_{j_{1}}(\mathbf{V}_{k})_{j_{1}i_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\sigma^{4}}{\lambda^{4}}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}

In addition,

\displaystyle\max_{j_{1}}\bigg\|\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{1}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{-1}\big)_{j_{1}i_{2}}\bigg(\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{mj_{1}}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\big(\mathbf{Z}_{k}\big)_{mj_{1}}^{2}\bigg)\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}.

By Bernstein’s inequality, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|\eta_{i_{1}i_{2}}|\geq t\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg(-c\min\bigg\{\frac{t^{2}}{\frac{\sigma^{4}}{\lambda^{4}}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}},\frac{t}{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p^{2}}}\bigg\}\bigg).

Let
t=C\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\sqrt{\log(p)}
. Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{|\eta_{i_{1}i_{2}}|\geq C\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg(-c\min\bigg\{C^{2}\log(p),\frac{Cp\sqrt{\log(p)}}{\mu_{0}}\bigg\}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim O(p^{-8}).

Taking a union bound over all
r^{2}
entries and noting that
r^{2}\lesssim\sqrt{p}
, it holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-7})
that

\displaystyle\alpha_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mu_{0}\frac{r^{3/2}}{p}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\sqrt{\log(p)}.

(37)

•

Step 5: Putting It All Together:
Combining (
32
), (
36
), and (
37
), we have that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{k}\widehat{\Gamma}^{(m)}\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma^{3}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{\lambda^{2}p}+\mu_{0}\frac{r^{3/2}}{p}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\sqrt{\log(p)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p}\bigg)

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
. We now complete the proof of the lemma. By Theorem 6.2 of
Higham (2008)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k})+\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})}\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{k}^{\top}-\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p}\bigg),

where we have used the observation that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})\geq\frac{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda},

(see the proof of
Theorem

16
), together with the assumption
\sigma/\sigma_{\min}=O(1)
. This bound holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
, which completes the proof.

∎

Appendix F
Proofs of Applications and Some More General Theorems

In this section we prove the results in
Section

5
. In
Section

F.1
we prove
Theorem

11
. Next, in
Section

F.2
we provide slightly more general statements of
Theorem

12
and
Theorem

13
; i.e.,
Theorem

22
and
Theorem

23
. We then prove
Theorem

22
and
Theorem

23
the subsequent two subsections. The proofs of these results rely heavily on the previous proofs.

F.1
Proof of
Theorem

11

Proof of
Theorem

11
.

Recall that throughout
r_{k}=O(1)
. We will also assume that
\mu_{0},\kappa=O(1)
, and that

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gg p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}.

(38)

We will verify these conditions the end of the proof. Without loss of generality we prove the result for
k=1
. For convenience we will suppress the dependence of
\mathbf{\Pi}_{1}
and
{\mathbf{U}}_{1}
on the index
k
.

By
Lemma

12
, it holds that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{1}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{1}\big)^{1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{1}^{\top}-\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{1}\big)^{1/2}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma}{\lambda}\left(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{p}\log(p)+\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{rp}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda}+\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r}\log(p)}{p}+\frac{\mu_{0}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p}\right)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma}{\lambda}\bigg(\frac{\sqrt{p}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda/\sigma}\bigg)

\displaystyle\ll\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\lambda}

since
\sigma/\sigma_{\min}=O(1)
. This implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{1}\big(\widehat{\Gamma}^{(m)}\big)^{1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{1}^{\top}-\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\right\|}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})}\to 0

almost surely, where we implicitly use the fact that
\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k})\gtrsim\frac{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}
. Consequently, we have the almost sure convergence

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{1}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{\Gamma}}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{W}}_{1}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\Gamma}^{(m)}_{k}\big)^{-1/2}-\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\|\to 0.

(39)

Next, by
Theorem

16
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}^{\top}\bigg(\Gamma^{(i)}+\Gamma^{(j)}\bigg)^{-1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j\cdot}-\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{i\cdot}+\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big)\to N(0,\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}),

(40)

where we note that the proof of
Theorem

16
reveals that rows
i
and
j
of
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}
are asymptotically independent.
Therefore, (
40
), (
39
), and the continuous mapping theorem imply

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\Gamma}^{(i)}+\widehat{\Gamma}^{(j)}\big)^{-1/2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j\cdot}-\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{i\cdot}+\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big)\to N(0,\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}).

Under
H_{0}
, by Proposition 2 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
it holds that
({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1})_{i\cdot}=({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1})_{j\cdot}
. Therefore, under the null it holds that

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\Gamma}^{(i)}+\widehat{\Gamma}^{(j)}\big)^{-1/2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)\to N(0,\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}),

and hence that

\displaystyle\widehat{T}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\|\big(\widehat{\Gamma}^{(i)}+\widehat{\Gamma}^{(j)}\big)^{-1/2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)\|^{2}\to\chi^{2}_{r_{1}}.

Next, under any alternative, Slutsky’s Theorem and
Eq.

39
imply

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\Gamma}^{(i)}

\displaystyle+\widehat{\Gamma}^{(j)}\big)^{-1/2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j\cdot}-\big(\widehat{\Gamma}^{(i)}+\widehat{\Gamma}^{(j)}\big)^{1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}^{\top}\bigg(\Gamma^{(i)}+\Gamma^{(j)}\bigg)^{-1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{i\cdot}+\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\to N(0,\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}),

which further implies that

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\Gamma}^{(i)}

\displaystyle+\widehat{\Gamma}^{(j)}\big)^{-1/2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)-\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}^{\top}\bigg(\Gamma^{(i)}+\Gamma^{(j)}\bigg)^{-1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{W}_{1}\big)_{i\cdot}+\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{W}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big)

\displaystyle\to N(0,\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}).

Therefore, the distribution of the random variable

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{\Gamma}^{(i)}+\widehat{\Gamma}^{(j)}\big)^{-1/2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)

is asymptotically equivalent to a Gaussian random variable with mean

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}^{\top}\bigg(\Gamma^{(i)}+\Gamma^{(j)}\bigg)^{-1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{i\cdot}+\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big).

Therefore, let
\eta_{ij}
denote this Gaussian random variable. By the Delta method, we have that

\displaystyle\widehat{T}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\|\eta_{ij}\|^{2},

where the equality is in distribution. It is straightforward to see that
\eta_{ij}^{2}
is a noncentral
\chi^{2}
distributed random variable with noncentrality parameter

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}^{\top}

\displaystyle\bigg(\Gamma^{(i)}+\Gamma^{(j)}\bigg)^{-1/2}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{i\cdot}+\big({\mathbf{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{W}}_{1}\big)_{j\cdot}\big)\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\big({\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)^{\top}\bigg(\Gamma^{(i)}+\Gamma^{(j)}\bigg)^{-1}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\big).

When this term converges to
\gamma<\infty
, we immediately obtain the result under the alternative. Therefore, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\big({\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)^{\top}\bigg(\Gamma^{(i)}+\Gamma^{(j)}\bigg)^{-1}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)\to\infty

(41)

whenever

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{S})}{\sigma}p\|\mathbf{\Pi}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\Pi}_{j\cdot}\|\to\infty.

(42)

We now verify this condition, as well the condition (
38
). To examine the SNR condition, we note that Lemma 1 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
shows that

\displaystyle\lambda\asymp\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{S})p^{3/2}

as long as
r
is bounded. Therefore, the signal-strength condition

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{S})^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg\frac{1}{p^{3/2}}\log(p)

is equivalent to the condition

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}\gg p^{3/2}\log(p),

which is guaranteed whenever

\displaystyle\lambda/\sigma\gg p^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)},

which is what is needed for
Theorem

19
. In addition, we note that the incoherence condition
\mu_{0}=O(1)
is guaranteed by Lemma 1 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
, as well as the fact that
\kappa=O(1)
. This verifies (
38
).

For the condition (
41
), we note that

\displaystyle\big({\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)^{\top}\bigg(\Gamma^{(i)}+\Gamma^{(j)}\bigg)^{-1}\big({\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\big)

\displaystyle\gtrsim\|{\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\|^{2}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}

Therefore, this term diverges as long as

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\|\gg\frac{\sigma}{\lambda}\asymp\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{S})p^{3/2}}

(43)

By Proposition 2 of
Agterberg and Zhang (2022)
, one has that
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}=\mathbf{\Pi}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}^{(\mathrm{pure})}
and that
{\mathbf{U}}^{(\mathrm{pure})}
has entries of order
\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\|

\displaystyle=\|\big(\mathbf{\Pi}{\mathbf{U}}^{(\mathrm{pure})}\big)_{i\cdot}-\big(\mathbf{\Pi}{\mathbf{U}}^{(\mathrm{pure})}\big)_{j\cdot}\|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\|\mathbf{\Pi}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\Pi}_{j\cdot}\|.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{S})}{\sigma}p\|\mathbf{\Pi}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{\Pi}_{j\cdot}\|

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{S})}{\sigma}p^{3/2}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{i\cdot}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j\cdot}\|

and therefore (
43
) holds whenever (
42
) holds, which completes the proof.
∎

F.2
Generalizations of Theorems
12
and
13

In this section we state generalizations of the applications in
Section

1.1
. The following result generalizes
Theorem

12
to the setting where
\mu_{0}
and
\kappa
are permitted to grow.

Theorem 22

(Generalization of
Theorem

12
)
.

Instate the conditions in
Theorem

14
, and suppose that

\displaystyle\mu_{0}^{2}\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}.

Let
J
be a given index set with
|J|=o(p^{1/6})
. . Define the
|J|\times|J|
matrix
S_{J}
via

\displaystyle(S_{J})_{\{i,j,k\},\{i^{\prime},j^{\prime},k^{\prime}\}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbb{I}_{\{i=i^{\prime}\}}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\Sigma_{1}^{(i)}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{j=j^{\prime}\}}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}\Sigma_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+i^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{k=k^{\prime}\}}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}\Sigma_{3}^{(k)}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i^{\prime}-1)p_{2}+^{\prime}},

where
\Sigma_{k}^{(m)}
is as in
Theorem

17
. Suppose
S_{J}
is invertible, and let
s^{2}_{\min}
denote its smallest eigenvalue. Suppose that

\displaystyle s_{\min}/\sigma

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{|J|^{3/2}\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},|J|^{3/2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)\sqrt{p}},|J|\frac{\mu_{0}^{5/2}r^{3/2}\kappa\log^{3/4}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{1/2}p^{3/4}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad|J|^{1/6}\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{3/2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{4/3}},|J|^{1/6}\frac{\kappa^{5/3}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{7/6}\log^{5/6}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{1/3}p^{5/6}}\bigg\}.

Let
\mathrm{C.I.}_{J}^{\alpha}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}})
denote the output of
Algorithm

5
.
Then it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J})\in\mathrm{C.I.}_{\alpha}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J})\bigg\}=1-\alpha-o(1).

The following result generalizes
Theorem

13
.

Theorem 23

(Generalization of
Theorem

13
)
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

14
, and suppose that
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
.
Suppose that

\displaystyle\min\bigg\{\|

\displaystyle e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2},\|e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2},\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\|e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2},\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2},\|e_{(i^{\prime}-1)p_{2}+j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)p^{3/2}},\frac{\kappa^{4}\mu_{0}^{6}r^{3}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\mu_{0}^{8}\kappa^{6}r^{3}\log^{2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{2}p}\bigg\}.

Let
\mathrm{C.I.}^{\alpha}_{\{ijk\},\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}})
denote the output of
Algorithm

6
.
Then it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}-\big(\mathcal{T}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\big)}{\widehat{s}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}\to N(0,1).

F.3
Proof of
Theorem

22

Proof of
Theorem

22
.

The proof is similar to the proof of
Theorem

20
. First, by the proof of
Theorem

17
, by applying the main expansion (
8
) to each entry separately, we have that with probability at least
1-O(|J|p^{-9})
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{J}

\displaystyle=\xi_{J}+O\bigg(|J|\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+|J|\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg),

where we set
\xi_{J}
as the random variable with entries
\xi_{ijk}
from
Lemma

10
. We will show that
\xi_{J}
is asymptotically Gaussian with covariance
S_{J}
, and we will demonstrate that
\widehat{S}_{J}
approximates
S_{J}
, which will yield the result.

•

Step 1: Limiting Covariance Structure and First-Order Approximation
:
Recall we define
s^{2}_{\min}
via

\displaystyle s^{2}_{\min}\coloneqq\lambda_{\min}(S_{J}),

If it holds that

\displaystyle s_{\min}

\displaystyle\gg|J|\sigma\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\},

(44)

then

\displaystyle S_{J}^{-1/2}\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J})

\displaystyle=S_{J}^{-1/2}\xi_{J}+o(1).

with probability at least
1-O(|J|p^{-9})
. It now remains to show that
\mathrm{Cov}(\xi_{J})\approx S_{J}
. By the proof of
Lemma

10
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk})

\displaystyle=s^{2}_{ijk}\bigg(1+O\big(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}\big)\bigg).

Therefore, it suffices to consider the covariance terms. Observe that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}\bigg(\xi_{ijk}\xi_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg({e}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}+e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}+e_{k}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}\bigg)

\displaystyle\times\bigg({e}_{i^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}+e_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{1}+i^{\prime}}+e_{k^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}\mathbf{V}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i^{\prime}-1)p_{2}+j^{\prime}}\bigg).

By a similar argument to Step 1 of the proof of
Lemma

10
, it holds that the cross term satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\bigg(e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg)\bigg(e_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{1}+i^{\prime}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O\bigg(\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\bigg(\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\|e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{1}+i^{\prime}}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle=O\bigg(\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\frac{r^{2}}{p^{2}}\bigg).

The other cross terms can be handled similarly. Hence, the only remaining terms are those such that
i=i^{\prime}
,
j=j^{\prime}
, or
k=k^{\prime}
. If
i=i^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg(

\displaystyle e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}\bigg)\bigg(e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}\bigg)\mathbb{I}_{\{i=i^{\prime}\}}

\displaystyle=e_{(j-1)p_{2}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\big(\Sigma^{(i)}\big)\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}},

with similar values if
j=j^{\prime}
or
k=k^{\prime}
; in particular, these are the entries of
S_{J}
by definition. Hence

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\xi_{J})

\displaystyle=S_{J}+O\bigg(\frac{|J|^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}s_{\min}^{2}\bigg),

(45)

provided that
s_{\min}^{2}\gg\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r/p
.

•

Step 2: Gaussian Approximation
: We now study the Gaussian approximation of the vector
S_{J}^{-1/2}\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J})
. We will apply Corollary 2.2 of
Shao and Zhang (2022)
. Define, for some sufficiently large constant
C
,

\displaystyle\Delta=\Delta^{(i)}

\displaystyle=C\left(|J|\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+|J|\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\right).

Note that
\mathrm{Cov}(\xi_{J})
is invertible with smallest eigenvalue at least
s^{2}_{\min}(1-o(1))
since
S_{J}
is invertible provided that
|J|^{2}\ll p/(\mu_{0}^{2}r)
. Consequently, we have that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\big(\mathrm{Cov}^{1/2}(\xi_{J})\big)

\displaystyle\gtrsim s_{\min}\gg\Delta

as long as

\displaystyle s_{\min}

\displaystyle\gg|J|\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\}.

(46)

Hence, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\xi_{J})^{-1/2}\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Cov}(\xi_{J})^{-1/2}\xi_{J}+\frac{\Delta}{s_{\min}}

with probability at least
1-O(|J|p^{-9})
. Let this event be denoted
\mathcal{A}
. By Corollary 2.2 of
Shao and Zhang (2022)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\mathrm{Cov}^{-1/2}\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J})\in A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim|J|^{1/2}\gamma+\frac{\Delta}{s_{\min}}\mathbb{E}\|\mathrm{Cov}^{-1/2}\xi_{J}\|+|J|p^{-9},

where
\gamma
is the sum of the third moments of the independent random variables in
\xi_{J}
(see step 2 of the proof of
Lemma

10
for the explicit definition in terms of the indices of
\xi_{J}
). A straightforward modification of the proof of
Lemma

10
shows that

\displaystyle\gamma

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}

as long as
s_{\min}\gg\Delta
, which is true by assumption. In addition, by subgaussianity, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\mathrm{Cov}^{-1/2}(\xi_{J})\xi_{J}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim|J|^{1/2}.

Consequently, we have that

\displaystyle\sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}}

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\mathrm{Cov}(\xi_{J})^{-1/2}\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J})\in A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+|J|p^{-9}+|J|^{1/2}\Delta

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{s_{\min}}\left(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\right),

which holds as long as

\displaystyle s_{\min}\gg|J|\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+|J|\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}.

(47)

Therefore, for any
A\in\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{S_{J}^{-1/2}\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J})\in A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\mathrm{Cov}(\xi_{J})^{-1/2}(\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J})\in{\rm Cov}(\xi_{J})^{-1/2}S_{J}^{1/2}A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z^{\prime}\in{\rm Cov}(\xi_{J})^{-1/2}S_{J}^{1/2}A\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z^{\prime}\in{\rm Cov}(\xi_{J})^{-1/2}S_{J}^{1/2}A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{s_{\min}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z^{\prime}\in{\rm Cov}(\xi_{J})^{-1/2}S_{J}^{1/2}A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A\bigg\}\bigg|

where
Z^{\prime}
is an independent
|J|
-dimensional standard Gaussian random variable. To bound the remaining term, by Theorem 1.3 of
Devroye et al. (2022)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{S_{J}^{-1/2}{\rm Cov}(\xi_{J})^{1/2}Z\in A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z^{\prime}\in A\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq d_{TV}(S_{J}^{-1/2}{\rm Cov}(\xi_{J})^{1/2}Z,Z^{\prime})

\displaystyle\lesssim\|I-S_{J}^{-1}{\rm Cov}(\xi_{J})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim|J|^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p},

where the final inequality holds by (
45
). Putting it all together, it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{S_{J}^{-1/2}\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J})\in A\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A\bigg\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{|J|^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}+\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{s_{\min}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

(48)

We note that this bound is only non-vacuous if these terms are smaller than one, which requires that

\displaystyle s_{\min}

\displaystyle\gg|J|^{3/2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\},

(49)

which is the stronger than the requirements (
44
),(
46
), and (
47
) by factors of
|J|
.

•

Step 3: Covariance Estimation
: We now consider the plug-in estimation of the covariance
S_{J}
. The proof of
Theorem

20
shows that with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})

\displaystyle|(s^{(i)})^{2}-(\widehat{s}^{(i)})^{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}},

with similar bounds for
s^{(j)}
and
s^{(k)}
, where the notation is defined in the proof of
Theorem

20
(see
Section

E.3
). A straightfoward modification of the same proof for the cross-terms reveals that with probability at least
1-O(|J|^{2}p^{-6})

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle S_{J}-\widehat{S}_{J}\|

\displaystyle\leq|J|^{2}\|S_{J}-\widehat{S}_{J}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim|J|^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}\frac{r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3}}+|J|^{2}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\ll s^{2}_{\min},

where the penultimate inequality holds as long as

\displaystyle s_{\min}^{2}

\displaystyle\gg|J|^{2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg\}.

(50)

Denote

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle\coloneqq|J|^{2}\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}\frac{r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3}}+|J|^{2}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg),

(51)

so that it holds that

\displaystyle\|S_{J}-\widehat{S}_{J}\|\lesssim M

with probability at least
1-O(|J|^{2}p^{-6})
. By Theorem 6.2 of
Higham (2008)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{S}_{J}^{1/2}-S_{J}^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(\widehat{S}_{J})+\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}(S_{J})}\|\widehat{S}_{J}-S_{J}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{s_{\min}}M

\displaystyle\ll s_{\min}

provided
M\ll s^{2}_{\min}
. Therefore, by Weyl’s inequality,
\widehat{S}_{J}
is invertible and

\displaystyle\|\bigg(S_{J}^{-1/2}-\widehat{S}_{J}^{-1/2}\bigg)(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J}-\mathcal{T}_{J})\|

\displaystyle\leq|J|\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}-\mathcal{T}\|_{\max}\|S_{J}^{-1/2}-\widehat{S}_{J}^{-1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq|J|\|S_{J}^{-1/2}(\widehat{S}_{J}^{1/2}-S_{J}^{1/2})\widehat{S}_{J}^{-1/2}\|\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}-\mathcal{T}\|_{\max}

\displaystyle\lesssim|J|\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}-\mathcal{T}\|_{\max}\frac{1}{s_{\min}^{2}}\|\widehat{S}_{J}^{1/2}-S_{J}^{1/2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim|J|\|\mathcal{\widehat{T}}-\mathcal{T}\|_{\max}\frac{1}{s_{\min}^{3}}M

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|M}{s_{\min}^{3}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=:\varepsilon,

where we note that the final inequality holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
by
Theorem

18
.

•

Step 4: Completing the Proof
: Just as in the proof of
Theorem

19
, for a convex set
A
denote
A^{\varepsilon}
as the
\varepsilon
-enlargment via

\displaystyle A^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\{x:d(x,A)\leq\varepsilon\}.

By Theorem 1.2 of
Raic\v (2019)
, if
A
is an isotropic
\mathbb{R}^{|J|}
-dimensional random gaussian vector, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg(Z\in A^{\varepsilon}\setminus A\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim|J|^{1/4}\varepsilon\lesssim|J|^{1/2}\varepsilon.

We are now prepared to complete the proof. Let
A_{\alpha}
denote the confidence region such that
\mathbb{P}(Z\in A_{\alpha})=1-\alpha
, where
Z\sim N(0,\mathbf{I}_{|J|})
. Then

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\bigg\{\mathrm{Vec}(\mathcal{T}_{J})\in\mathrm{C.I.}_{\alpha}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J})\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\widehat{S}_{J}^{-1/2}\bigg(\mathrm{Vec}\big(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J}-\mathcal{T}_{J}\big)\bigg)\in\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{S_{J}^{-1/2}\bigg(\mathrm{Vec}\big(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J}-\mathcal{T}_{J}\big)\bigg)\in\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}\bigg\}\cap\bigg\{\bigg|\big(S_{J}^{-1/2}-\widehat{S}_{J}^{-1/2}\big)\bigg(\mathrm{Vec}\big(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J}-\mathcal{T}_{J}\big)\bigg)\bigg|\leq\varepsilon\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\big(S_{J}^{-1/2}-\widehat{S}_{J}^{-1/2}\big)\bigg(\mathrm{Vec}\big(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J}-\mathcal{T}_{J}\big)\bigg)\big|>\varepsilon\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{S_{J}^{-1/2}\bigg(\mathrm{Vec}\big(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J}-\mathcal{T}_{J}\big)\bigg)\in\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|+O(|J|^{2}p^{-6})

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{S_{J}^{-1/2}\bigg(\mathrm{Vec}\big(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{J}-\mathcal{T}_{J}\big)\bigg)\in\mathcal{A}_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}\bigg\}\bigg|+\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+O(|J|^{2}p^{-6})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{|J|^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}+\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{s_{\min}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A_{\alpha}^{\varepsilon}\bigg\}-\mathbb{P}\bigg\{Z\in A_{\alpha}\bigg\}\bigg|+O(|J|^{2}p^{-6})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{|J|^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}+\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{s_{\min}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}(Z\in A^{\varepsilon}_{\alpha}\setminus A_{\alpha})+O(|J|^{2}p^{-6})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{|J|^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}+\frac{|J|^{3/2}}{s_{\min}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+|J|^{1/2}\varepsilon+O(|J|^{2}p^{-6}),

where we have used (
48
).
In order to complete the proof we need that this final bound is of order
o(1)
. For the first two terms to be
o(1)
, we see that
J
needs to satisfy

\displaystyle|J|

\displaystyle\ll\min\bigg\{p^{1/6},\frac{p^{1/2}}{\mu_{0}r^{1/2}}\bigg\};

This is guaranteed if
|J|=o(p^{1/6})
, since we assume that
\mu_{0}^{2}r\lesssim\sqrt{p}
, so that
\frac{p^{1/2}}{\mu_{0}r^{1/2}}\gtrsim p^{1/4}
. This also is sufficient for the probability terms to be
o(1)
.

We now translate the conditions on
s_{\min}
. First we collect all our requirements from the previous steps. From steps 1 and 2, (
49
) requires that

\displaystyle s_{\min}

\displaystyle\gg|J|^{3/2}\sigma\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\}.

In addition, from (
50
), we require

\displaystyle s_{\min}^{2}

\displaystyle\gg|J|^{2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg\}.

(52)

Finally, we also require that
|J|^{1/2}\varepsilon=o(1)
, which translates to the condition

\displaystyle s_{\min}^{3}

\displaystyle\gg\sigma|J|^{3/2}M\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p},\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\}.

Recalling the definition of
M
in (
51
), this condition is equivalent to the condition

\displaystyle s_{\min}^{3}

\displaystyle\gg\sigma|J|^{7/2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p},\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{3}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\times\bigg(\mu_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p}s_{\min}^{2}+\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}\frac{r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}}+\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg).

When
|J|=o(p^{1/6})
and
\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}\lesssim p^{1/4}
this condition is satisfied when

\displaystyle s_{\min}^{3}

\displaystyle\gg\sigma|J|^{7/2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}\sqrt{r\log(p)}}{p},\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\asymp\sigma^{3}|J|^{7/2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{4}r^{2}\log(p)}{p^{4}},\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{p^{4}},\frac{\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{6}\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)p^{5/2}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\frac{\kappa^{3}\mu_{0}^{7}r^{7/2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)p^{7/2}},\frac{\kappa^{4}\mu_{0}^{6}r^{7/2}\log^{2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)p^{7/2}},\frac{\kappa^{5}\mu_{0}^{9}r^{2}\log^{5/2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{2}p^{5/2}}\bigg\}

Putting it together and putting terms on the same scale, we see that we have the three conditions

\displaystyle s_{\min}/\sigma

\displaystyle\gg|J|^{3/2}\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},\frac{\sigma\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle s_{\min}/\sigma

\displaystyle\gg|J|\max\bigg\{\frac{\mu_{0}^{3/2}r^{3/4}\log^{1/4}(p)}{p^{3/2}},\frac{\kappa^{1/2}\mu_{0}r^{3/4}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},\frac{\mu_{0}^{5/2}r^{3/2}\kappa\log^{3/4}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{1/2}p^{3/4}}\bigg\};

\displaystyle s_{\min}/\sigma

\displaystyle\gg|J|^{1/6}\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa^{1/3}\mu_{0}^{4/3}r^{2/3}\log^{1/3}(p)}{p^{4/3}},\frac{\kappa^{2/3}\mu_{0}r^{2/3}\log^{1/2}(p)}{p^{4/3}},\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{1/6}\log^{2/3}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{1/3}p^{5/6}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{7/3}r^{7/6}\log^{1/2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{1/3}p^{7/6}},\frac{\kappa^{4/3}\mu_{0}^{2}r^{7/6}\log^{2/3}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{1/3}p^{7/6}},\frac{\kappa^{5/3}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{2/3}\log^{5/6}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{2/3}p^{5/6}}\bigg\}.

Removing redundant conditions shows that we require

\displaystyle s_{\min}/\sigma

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{|J|^{3/2}\frac{\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}},|J|^{3/2}\frac{\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)\sqrt{p}},|J|\frac{\mu_{0}^{5/2}r^{3/2}\kappa\log^{3/4}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{1/2}p^{3/4}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad|J|^{1/6}\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{3/2}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{4/3}},|J|^{1/6}\frac{\kappa^{5/3}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{7/6}\log^{5/6}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{1/3}p^{5/6}}\bigg\}.

which holds under the conditions of
Theorem

22
.

∎

F.4
Proof of
Theorem

23

Proof of
Theorem

23
.

Similar to the previous proof, by the proof of
Theorem

17
, by (
8
) it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{ijk}=\xi_{ijk}+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
, where
\xi_{ijk}
is defined as in
Lemma

10
. A similar expansion holds for
\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}-\mathcal{T}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}
, which demonstrates that

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}-(\mathcal{T}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}})

\displaystyle=\xi_{ijk}-\mathcal{\xi}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

Our proof now proceeds in a similar manner to
Theorem

22
.

•

Step 1: Limiting Variance Structure
: We first calculate the variance of
\xi_{ijk}
. However, through precisely the same analysis as in step 1 of
Theorem

22
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}\bigg(\xi_{ijk}\xi_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\{i=i^{\prime}\}}e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{(i)}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{1}^{\top}e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{j=j^{\prime}\}}e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{2}^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{(j)}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{2}^{\top}e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+i^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{I}_{\{k=k^{\prime}\}}e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{3}^{\top}\widehat{\Sigma}^{(k)}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{3}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{3}^{\top}e_{(i^{\prime}-1)p_{2}+j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad+O\bigg(\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\bigg[s^{2}_{ijk}+s^{2}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\bigg]\bigg).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\xi_{ijk}-\xi_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}})

\displaystyle=s^{2}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}\bigg(1+O\bigg(\mu_{0}^{2}\frac{r}{p}\bigg)\bigg).

•

Step 2: Gaussian Approximation and Variance Approximation
:
Define, for some sufficiently large constant
C
,

\displaystyle\Delta=\Delta^{(i)}=C\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\mu_{0}^{3}r^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(p)}}{p^{3/2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu_{0}^{4}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log(p)}{\lambda\sqrt{p}}\bigg).

Observe that we have the expansion

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}-(\mathcal{T}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}})}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{ijk}-\xi_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}+\frac{\Delta}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}},

which holds with probability at least
1-O(p^{-9})
. By modifying the proof of
Lemma

10
, it is straightforward to demonstrate that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi_{ijk}-\xi_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}\bigg\}-\Phi(t)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{p\log(p)}}+\frac{C_{2}\mu_{0}^{2}r}{p}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\eta.

Hence it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}-(\mathcal{T}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}})}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}\leq t\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi_{ijk}-\xi_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}\leq t+\frac{\Delta}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}\bigg\}+C_{3}p^{-9}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi\bigg\{t+\frac{\Delta}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}\bigg\}+C_{3}p^{-9}+\eta

\displaystyle\leq\Phi(t)+\frac{\Delta}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}+C_{3}p^{-9}+\eta

\displaystyle=\Phi(t)+o(1),

where the final bounds holds as long as
s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}\gg\Delta
, which we justify at the end of the proof.
In addition, it is straightforward to modify the proof of
Theorem

20
to demonstrate that as long as

\displaystyle s^{2}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg\}

(53)

it holds that
\widehat{s}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}=s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}(1+o(1))
with probability at least
1-O(p^{-6})
. We will check this condition at the end of the proof.
Denote the region

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}}{\widehat{s}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}-z_{\alpha/2},\frac{\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{ijk}-\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}}}{\widehat{s}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}+z_{\alpha/2}\bigg).

Consequently, by similar manipulations to the end of the proof of
Theorem

20
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{(\mathcal{T}_{ijk}-\mathcal{T}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}})}{s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}}\in\mathcal{R}_{\alpha}\bigg\}-(1-\alpha)\bigg|

\displaystyle=o(1).

This completes the proof, provided we can justify all of the conditions on
s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}
.

•

Step 3: Checking Conditions
: Observe that (
53
) requires that

\displaystyle s^{2}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{3}\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{\lambda p^{3/2}}\bigg\}.

In addition, we require that
s_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}\gg\Delta
. It is sufficient to require that

\displaystyle s^{2}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{4}\mu_{0}^{6}r^{3}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\sigma^{4}\mu_{0}^{8}\kappa^{6}r^{4}\log^{2}(p)}{\lambda^{2}p}\bigg\}.

Observe that
s^{2}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}
satisfies

\displaystyle s^{2}_{\{ijk\}\{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}k^{\prime}\}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\sigma^{2}\min\bigg\{\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|,\|e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|,

\displaystyle\quad\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|,\|e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|,\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|,\|e_{(i^{\prime}-1)p_{2}+j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|\bigg\}.

To see this, note that no matter how many overlapping indices there, there is always one set of indices that are not overlapping, and hence the variance can be lower bounded by at least one of

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}^{2}\times\bigg\{

\displaystyle\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2},\|e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\quad\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2},\|e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2},\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2},\|e_{(i^{\prime}-1)p_{2}+j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg\}.

Therefore, as long as

\displaystyle\min\bigg\{

\displaystyle\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2},\|e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2},\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\quad\|e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2},\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2},\|e_{(i^{\prime}-1)p_{2}+j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{\frac{\kappa\mu_{0}^{2}r^{3/2}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\mu_{0}^{5}r^{3}\kappa^{2}\log^{3/2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)p^{3/2}},\frac{\kappa^{4}\mu_{0}^{6}r^{3}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{\mu_{0}^{8}\kappa^{6}r^{3}\log^{2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)^{2}p}\bigg\}.

we see all our results continue to hold. In particular, when
\kappa,\mu_{0}=O(1)
, it is sufficient to have that

\displaystyle\min\bigg\{

\displaystyle\|e_{(j-1)p_{3}+k}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2},\|e_{(j^{\prime}-1)p_{3}+k^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}\|^{2},\|e_{(k-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\quad\|e_{(k^{\prime}-1)p_{1}+i}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{2}\|^{2},\|e_{(i-1)p_{2}+j}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2},\|e_{(i^{\prime}-1)p_{2}+j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{V}_{3}\|^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\gg\max\bigg\{\frac{r^{3}\log(p)}{p^{3}},\frac{r^{3}\log^{3/2}(p)}{(\lambda/\sigma)p^{3/2}}\bigg\},

which is precisely the condition in
Theorem

13
.

∎

Appendix G
Proofs of Lower Bounds

In this section we prove all of our lower bound results. In the subsequent section we prove both
Theorem

4
and
Theorem

8
, and in
Section

G.2
we prove
Theorems

5
and
9
.

G.1
Proofs of
Theorem

4
and
Theorem

8

In this section we prove
Theorem

4
and
Theorem

8
. We prove both results simultaneously in a self-contained manner.

Proofs of
Theorem

4
and
Theorem

8
.

Without loss of generality we assume that
\sigma^{2}\equiv\sigma^{2}_{\min}
, since otherwise we only increase the variance. In addition, we assume that the core tensor
\mathcal{C}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{1}\times r_{2}\times r_{3}}
is known, since not knowing it will also only increase the variance.

Next, by the Cramer-Rao lower bound, for any unbiased estimator
(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{2},\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{3})
of the parameter
({\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3})
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{2},\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{3})

\displaystyle\succcurlyeq\mathcal{I}^{-1},

where
\mathcal{I}
is defined via

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\nabla_{{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\log\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}};{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3})\bigg]\bigg[\nabla_{{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\log\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{\widehat{T}};{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3})\bigg]^{\top},

By the delta method, for any unbiased estimator
\mathcal{\check{T}}_{ijk}
of
\mathcal{T}_{ijk}
it holds that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\mathcal{\check{T}}_{ijk})\geq\nabla_{{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{ijk}^{\top}\mathcal{I}^{-1}\nabla_{{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{ijk},

where
\nabla_{{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{ijk}
is the gradient of
\mathcal{T}_{ijk}
with respect to the (vectorized) parameter
({\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3})
.
We now proceed in several steps.

•

Step 1: Calculating the Relevant Quantities
:
First, we note that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\mathcal{T}_{ijk}}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{l_{1},s}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{l_{1},s}}\sum_{a,b,c}\mathcal{C}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{ia}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{kc}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{i=l_{1}}\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{sbc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{kc}.

(54)

A similar calculation can be made for the other modes, with appropriate replacements.

We now calculate the Fisher information matrix. To do so, we will use the convention that the vectorized matrices
{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},
and
{\mathbf{U}}_{3}
are in the order such that they are first indexed by row then by column, which will be a useful convention later on. We note that

\displaystyle\log f(\mathcal{\widehat{T}};{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3},\mathcal{C})

\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{2\sigma^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}\bigg(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}-\sum_{a,b,c}\mathcal{C}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{i_{1}a}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)^{2};

and hence that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{la^{\prime}}}\log f(\mathcal{\widehat{T}};{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3},\mathcal{C})

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{la^{\prime}}}\frac{-1}{2\sigma^{2}}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}\bigg(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}-\sum_{a,b,c}\mathcal{C}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{i_{1}a}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{\sigma^{2}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\bigg(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{li_{2}i_{3}}-\sum_{a,b,c}\mathcal{C}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{la}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\frac{\partial}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{la^{\prime}}}\bigg(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{li_{2}i_{3}}-\sum_{a,b,c}\mathcal{C}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{la}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\bigg(\mathcal{\widehat{T}}_{li_{2}i_{3}}-\sum_{a,b,c}\mathcal{C}_{abc}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{la}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a^{\prime}bc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\mathcal{Z}_{li_{2}i_{3}}\bigg(\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a^{\prime}bc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\Bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{l_{1}a_{1}}}\log f(\mathcal{\widehat{T}};{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3},\mathcal{C})\frac{\partial}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{l_{2}a_{2}}}\log f(\mathcal{\widehat{T}};{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3},\mathcal{C})\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{4}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\mathcal{Z}_{l_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}\bigg(\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{1}bc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)\Bigg]\Bigg[\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\mathcal{Z}_{l_{2}i_{2}i_{3}}\bigg(\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{2}bc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\mathbb{I}_{l_{1}=l_{2}}\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\bigg(\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{1}bc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{2}bc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\mathbb{I}_{l_{1}=l_{2}}\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{1}bc}\mathcal{C}_{a_{2}bc}

(55)

where the second inequality uses the independence of
\mathcal{Z}_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}
, and the final inequality is by orthonormality. Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\frac{\partial}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{l_{1}a_{1}}}\log f(\mathcal{\widehat{T}};{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3},\mathcal{C})\frac{\partial}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{l_{2}b_{1}}}\log f(\mathcal{\widehat{T}};{\mathbf{U}}_{1},{\mathbf{U}}_{2},{\mathbf{U}}_{3},\mathcal{C})\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{4}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\sum_{i_{2},i_{3}}\mathcal{Z}_{l_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}\bigg(\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{1}bc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)\Bigg]\Bigg[\sum_{i_{1},i_{3}}\mathcal{Z}_{i_{1}l_{2}i_{3}}\bigg(\sum_{a,c}\mathcal{C}_{ab_{1}c}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{i_{1}a}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\sum_{i_{3}}\bigg(\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{1}bc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{l_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{a,c}\mathcal{C}_{ab_{1}c}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{l_{1}a}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)

(56)

Similar derivations can be obtained for
{\mathbf{U}}_{2}
and
{\mathbf{U}}_{3}
.

Define the
r_{1}
vectors
\{\mathbf{c}_{a_{i}}\}_{i=1}^{r_{1}}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{2}r_{3}}
by setting its
(b,c)
entry equal to
\mathcal{C}_{a_{i}bc}
, and define the
r_{2}
and
r_{3}
vectors
\{\mathbf{c}_{b_{j}}\}_{j=1}^{r_{2}}
and
\{\mathbf{c}_{c_{k}}\}_{k=1}^{r_{3}}
similarly. With this definition, we note that (
55
) is simply the equation for the sample gram matrix for the
r_{1}
vectors
\{\mathbf{c}_{a_{i}}\}_{i=1}^{r_{1}}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{2}r_{3}}
(with similar observations for the other two modes). Therefore, abusing notation slightly, let
\mathbf{I}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{p_{1}r_{1}\times p_{1}r_{1}}
be the block-diagonal matrix with
r_{1}\times r_{1}
blocks given by this sample gram matrix, and let
\mathbf{I}_{2}
and
\mathbf{I}_{3}
be defined similarly. With these notations in place, we see that
\mathcal{I}
can be written in the following form:

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}\mathcal{I}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{I}_{1}&0&0\\
0&\mathbf{I}_{2}&0\\
0&0&\mathbf{I}_{3}\end{pmatrix}+\begin{pmatrix}0&\mathbf{H}_{12}&\mathbf{H}_{13}\\
\mathbf{H}_{12}^{\top}&0&\mathbf{H}_{23}\\
\mathbf{H}_{13}^{\top}&\mathbf{H}_{23}^{\top}&0\end{pmatrix},

where
\mathbf{H}_{12},\mathbf{H}_{13}
and
\mathbf{H}_{23}
are of appropriate dimension with entries given by the identity in (
56
).

•

Step 2: Three Tensor Algebra Identities
:
We now simplify the expressions in (
54
),
(
55
) and (
56
).
We recall from (
54
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\mathcal{T}_{ijk}}{\partial({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{i,s}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{sbc}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{kc}

\displaystyle=\sum_{b,c}\bigg(\sum_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{i_{1}s}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{kc}

\displaystyle=\sum_{b,c}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{kc}\bigg(\sum_{i_{1}}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{i_{1}s}\sum_{i_{2}i_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{b,c}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{jb}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{kc}\bigg({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3})\bigg)_{s,(b,c)}

\displaystyle=\Bigg[{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{T}_{1}({\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3})({\mathbf{U}}_{2}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{3})^{\top}\Bigg]_{s,(j-1)p_{3}+k}

\displaystyle=\Bigg[{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{2}}\Bigg]_{s,(j-1)p_{3}+k}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{\top}\bigg)_{s,(j-1)p_{3}+k}.

(57)

Here we have denoted the index
(b,c)
to be the matricization index corresponding to the
s,b,c
element of the underlying tensor.

We now consider the form of the blocks in the definition of
\mathbf{I}_{1}
. Recall that each block is given by the matrix whose
(a_{1},a_{2})
entry is equal to

\displaystyle\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{1}bc}\mathcal{C}_{a_{2}bc}.

We have that

\displaystyle\sum_{b,c}\mathcal{C}_{a_{1}bc}\mathcal{C}_{a_{2}bc}

\displaystyle=\sum_{b,c}\bigg(\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{i_{1}a_{1}}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}c}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{j_{1}a_{2}}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{j_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{j_{3}c}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{b,c}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{i_{1}a_{1}}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{i_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{i_{3}}\bigg[\sum_{j_{1}}({\mathbf{U}}_{1})_{j_{1}a_{2}}\sum_{j_{2}j_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{j_{1}j_{2}j_{3}}({\mathbf{U}}_{2})_{j_{2}b}({\mathbf{U}}_{3})_{j_{3}c}\bigg]
